### [1] [Axion Quality from Exact Proton Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01712)
*Joe Davighi,Admir Greljo,Xavier Ponce Díaz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离散的、无反常的 \mathbb Z_9 规范对称性可在深红外区域存续，恰好禁止质子衰变。该 \mathbb Z_9 可由轻子味非普遍的 U(1)_X 通过 Higgs 机制破缺产生，该高能 seesaw 机制能产生现实的味混合，并预言额外的轻戈尔斯通玻色子。加入无反常的手征重夸克扇区可将其转化为 QCD 轴子。相同的选择定则既禁止质子衰变，也禁止低于高维度 d_{\rm PQ}\gtrsim10 的 PQ 破缺算符。作者构建了 PQ 破缺发生在暴胀之前或之后的显式模型，并识别出热轻子生成仍可行的参数空间子区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子衰变是超出标准模型物理的关键驱动之一，但传统离散对称性虽能禁止质子衰变却面临量子引力/反常问题。作者寻求一种既能在深红外精确禁止质子衰变、又自然具有高“质量”的离散规范对称性，并能同时解释中微子质量与 QCD 强 CP 问题。

Method: 利用轻子味非普遍的 U(1)_X 通过高能 seesaw 机制产生中微子质量，经 Higgs 破缺后涌现出离散 \mathbb Z_9 规范对称性，该对称性恰好禁止质子衰变并给出高质量轴子解。作者构建了两类显式模型（PQ 破缺在暴胀前或后），详细分析避免稳定重衰变物与消除弦-墙网络对重夸克谱的约束，并计算热轻子生成与轴子暗物质共存的条件。

Result: 构造了成功的显式模型：当 PQ 破缺在暴胀前发生时不需担心遗传缺陷问题；当发生在暴胀后时，则需要严格控制重夸克谱以避免稳定重残余物和消除弦-墙网络。在受限制的参数空间区域中热轻子生成仍可行。对于 d_{\rm PQ}\geq13 的情形，高保真度轴子可构成全部暗物质并与热轻子生成共存。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，离散 \mathbb Z_9 对称性能在禁止质子衰变的同时自然增强 PQ 对称性的保真度，统一解决质子稳定性、强 CP 问题、中微子质量与暗物质等多个难题；轴子保真度（PQ 破缺算符维度）与热轻子生成的可行性之间存在明确的权衡关系。

Abstract: A discrete, anomaly-free $\mathbb Z_9$ gauge symmetry may persist to the deep infrared, exactly forbidding proton decay. This $\mathbb Z_9$ can emerge from Higgsing a lepton-flavour non-universal $U(1)_X$ that generates realistic neutrino masses and mixings through a high-scale seesaw and predicts an additional light Goldstone. Adding an anomaly-free chiral heavy-quark sector turns this mode into the QCD axion. The same selection rules that ban proton decay also forbid PQ-violating operators below a suitably high dimension, $d_{\rm PQ}\gtrsim10$, including operators involving the heavy quarks that radiatively match onto the scalar potential. We construct explicit models viable when PQ breaking occurs before or after inflation; in the latter case, avoiding stable heavy relics and eliminating the string-wall network tightly constrain the heavy-quark spectrum. We identify restricted regions of parameter space where thermal leptogenesis remains viable and, for $d_{\rm PQ}\geq13$, can coexist with a high-quality axion constituting all of the dark matter.

</details>


### [2] [SN1987A constraints on the neutrino-dark-fermion interaction from resonant scattering with C$ν$B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01716)
*Christina Gao,Ke Hu,Kun-Feng Lyu,Vincent Zou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用SN1987A超新星中微子数据约束光标量介导的中微子自相互作用。当标量粒子同时耦合到暗区费米子时，超新星中微子可与宇宙中微子背景共振湮灭为不可见的暗辐射，造成地球上的中微子通量衰减。研究发现新物理极限不能独立于重味发射、味道转换和耦合结构单独给出，但未来Hyper-Kamiokande高统计量事件可恢复有效的上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子自相互作用（由光标量介导）被认为是解决宇宙学张力的有力候选方案，并可能自然出现在中微子质量产生机制中。当该标量还耦合到暗区轻费米子时，超新星中微子会与宇宙中微子背景发生共振湮灭为暗辐射，导致到达地球的通量降低。作者希望利用这种"通量衰减"现象，从SN1987A数据对中微子-标量耦合强度给出约束。

Method: 在贝叶斯框架下，作者分析了两种超新星中微子发射模型（参数化模型和二维流体动力学模拟）以及两种耦合类型（质量无关和质量正比于质量）。通过共振湮灭过程引起的通量衰减来约束中微子-标量耦合参数空间，并与SN1987A观测数据进行比较。同时对Hyper-Kamiokande未来高统计量爆发事件进行了预测性分析。

Result: 结果表明，SN1987A的新物理极限不能独立于重味发射、味道转换或耦合结构单独引用，即这些模型和参数的选取对最终约束具有显著影响。此外，对未来Hyper-Kamiokande观测的预测显示，即使包含味道转换效应，高统计量的超新星爆发数据也能恢复有意义的上限约束。

Conclusion: SN1987A数据对中微子-暗辐射相互作用给出的约束具有强模型依赖性，需谨慎引用。未来Hyper-Kamiokande等下一代实验的高统计量超新星爆发观测，有望为这一物理场景提供更为可靠和稳健的约束能力。

Abstract: Neutrino self-interactions mediated by a light scalar offer a compelling resolution to cosmological tensions and may naturally arise in neutrino mass generation mechanisms. When the scalar also couples to a light dark-sector fermion, supernova neutrinos can resonantly annihilate with the cosmic neutrino background (C$ν$B) into invisible dark radiation, depleting the flux en route to Earth. We use this depletion to constrain the neutrino-scalar coupling from the SN1987A data. Within a Bayesian framework we analyze the data for two supernova neutrino emission models, a parameterized model and a 2D hydrodynamic simulation, and for two coupling types, a mass-independent one and a mass-proportional one. Our results show that new-physics limits from SN1987A cannot be quoted independently of the heavy-flavor emission, the flavor conversion, or the coupling structure. Finally, we forecast that a future high-statistics burst recorded by Hyper-Kamiokande would restore a meaningful upper bound even when flavor conversion is included.

</details>


### [3] [Primordial Black Hole Assisted Dirac Leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01717)
*Marco Manno,Anish Ghoshal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了蒸发中的原初黑洞（PBHs）如何影响Dirac轻子生成机制。通过求解包含热产生、Hawking辐射、熵注入等过程的耦合Boltzmann方程组，发现Hawking辐射可以在热产生低效的区域补充中介粒子X的丰度，使其CP破坏衰变增强重子不对称性，将可行的中介粒子质量范围从约10^9 GeV扩展到约10^6 GeV，但同时PBHs也会向右手征中微子注入能量并贡献ΔN_eff。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac轻子生成为重子不对称性提供了与Majorana方案根本不同的途径——总轻子数守恒，但标准模型和右手征中微子扇区存储大小相等、符号相反的不对称性，仅可见部分被电弱sphaleron过程处理。此前尚无专门研究蒸发原初黑洞（PBHs）对这一机制影响的工作，而PBHs作为Hawking辐射源可能显著改变粒子产生动力学。

Method: 在最小带电标量中介粒子（X）的框架下，构建并求解包含以下项的耦合Boltzmann方程组：热产生过程、X和右手征中微子的Hawking发射、PBH对膨胀速率的贡献以及熵注入效应。通过代表性benchmark参数扫描，系统评估PBHs对最终重子不对称性的影响，并与观测值及ΔN_eff约束进行比较。

Result: Hawking辐射可以在热产生低效的参数区域有效补充X的丰度，其后续CP破坏衰变能增强重子不对称性。在代表性参数点下，PBHs将可行区域从中介粒子质量约10^9 GeV扩展到约10^6 GeV，并能补偿更小的有效CP不对称性。然而该效应并非普适：在热Dirac轻子生成已高效的区域，PBHs反而会降低最终不对称性。PBH蒸发还在右手征中微子扇区沉积能量并贡献ΔN_eff。参数扫描中，复现观测重子不对称性的部分区域位于Planck参考值ΔN_eff=0.284以下，但高于CMB-S4预计灵敏度ΔN_eff=0.06。

Conclusion: 原初黑洞蒸发对Dirac轻子生成机制的影响是双向且参数依赖的：一方面可以扩展模型可行参数空间并容忍更小的CP不对称性，另一方面在原本高效率区域会抑制重子不对称性。该机制的可观测印记体现在ΔN_eff上，其数值介于Planck约束与CMB-S4灵敏度之间，为未来的宇宙微波背景实验提供了可检验的预言窗口。

Abstract: Dirac leptogenesis offers a qualitatively different route to the baryon asymmetry: total lepton number is conserved, but equal and opposite asymmetries are stored in the Standard Model and right handed neutrino sectors, with only the visible component processed by electroweak sphalerons. We present the first dedicated study of how evaporating primordial black holes (PBHs) modify this mechanism. In a minimal charged-scalar mediator setup, we solve the coupled Boltzmann system including thermal production, Hawking emission of the mediator $X$ and of right handed neutrinos, the PBH contribution to the expansion rate, and entropy injection. We find that Hawking emission can populate $X$ in regions where thermal production is inefficient, allowing its subsequent CP-violating decays to enhance the baryon asymmetry. For representative benchmarks, PBHs extend the successful region from mediator masses around $10^9\,{\rm GeV}$ down to about $10^6\,{\rm GeV}$ and can compensate for smaller effective CP asymmetries. This effect is not universal: where thermal Dirac leptogenesis is already efficient, PBHs can instead reduce the final asymmetry. Their evaporation also deposits energy in the right handed neutrino sector and contributes to $ΔN_{\rm eff}$. In the PBH parameter scan, part of the region that reproduces the observed baryon asymmetry lies below the Planck reference level $ΔN_{\rm eff}=0.284$ and above the projected CMB-S4 sensitivity $ΔN_{\rm eff}=0.06$.

</details>


### [4] [Electromagnetic tails of $B$-meson light-cone distribution amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01718)
*Max Ferré,Matthias Neubert*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在重夸克有效理论框架下研究B介子衰变常数和光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）的电磁修正。QED效应将LCDAs的支持扩展到负光锥动量区域，作者阐明了该区域与对电磁末态辐射敏感的QED广义衰变常数的关系，并推导了QED广义LCDAs在负动量渐近区域的模型无关表达式，最后提出符合这些约束的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B介子物理中，电磁（QED）修正对精确理解衰变常数和光锥分布振幅至关重要。在纯QCD框架下，B介子LCDAs定义在正光锥动量范围；引入QED后支持扩展到负区域，需要澄清这一新区域与可观测量的关系，并建立其与纯QCD量的联系。

Method: 在重夸克有效理论框架下包含QED效应，系统研究B介子QED广义LCDAs Φ±(ω,μ)。推导其在负动量渐近大区域与两类和三类粒子LCDAs积分的模型无关表达；利用已知的单圈反常维数检验Φ+与重整化群方程的一致性，并研究其演化行为。

Result: 推导出Φ±(ω,μ)在大负动量区域的渐近行为表达式，以纯QCD中两粒子和三粒子LCDAs的积分形式给出；验证了Φ+的结果满足重整化群方程，并利用单圈反常维数研究了其演化。

Conclusion: 建立了QED修正下B介子LCDAs负动量区域与广义衰变常数的联系，给出了模型无关的渐近表达式，并构建了与理论约束一致的唯象模型。

Abstract: We investigate electromagnetic corrections to the $B$-meson decay constant and light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) within the framework of heavy-quark effective theory. The inclusion of QED effects extends the support of the LCDAs to negative values of the light-cone momentum. We clarify the relation between this region and QED-generalized decay constants sensitive to electromagnetic final-state radiation. We derive model-independent expressions for the asymptotic behavior in the large negative-momentum region of the QED-generalized LCDAs $Φ_\pm(ω,μ)$ in terms of integrals over the two- and three-particle LCDAs defined in pure QCD. We show that our result for $Φ_+$ is consistent with the renormalization-group equation satisfied by this object and study its evolution using the known one-loop anomalous dimension. Finally, we propose a phenomenological model consistent with these constraints

</details>


### [5] [Tremaine-Gunn Control: Evading Bounds on Light Fermion Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01727)
*Joel Barir,Diego Blas,Anubhav Mathur,Tomer Volansky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明，暗扇区相互作用可以显著削弱Pauli不相容原理对费米子暗物质质量施加的模型无关下限（Tremaine-Gunn约束）。通过吸引性的有限程标量Yukawa力平衡简并压，eV量级的费米子暗物质可以形成具有矮星系特征尺寸和密度的束缚结构，从而规避传统质量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Pauli不相容原理为星系中费米子暗物质的质量设定了模型无关的下界（Tremaine-Gunn约束），极大地限制了轻费米子暗物质的可能性。作者希望探究暗扇区相互作用是否能够从根本上改变这一结论，为超轻费米子暗物质开辟新的理论路径。

Method: 作者提出引入一种比引力强得多的吸引性有限程标量Yukawa力来平衡简并压，使eV级费米子能够形成矮星系尺度与密度的束缚结构。该相互作用作用于矮星系尺度但在更大尺度上被屏蔽；作者推导了由此产生的暗物质流体的演化方程，并论证了可行的宇宙学历史：结构先通过引力形成，新的相互作用在晚期才被激活。作为概念验证，他们构造了由单一晚期相变产生所需动力学的具体模型。

Result: 作者成功证明暗扇区相互作用可以实质性地削弱常规相位空间限制，使得eV量级的超轻费米子暗物质能够形成与矮星系相称的结构；并提供了一个包含单一晚期相变的可行宇宙学模型作为概念验证。

Conclusion: 这些结果为极轻费米子暗物质提供了新途径，并证实暗扇区相互作用能够从本质上改变传统Paul i相空间极限的适用性与结论。

Abstract: Pauli exclusion is often regarded as imposing a model-independent lower bound on the mass of fermionic dark matter in galaxies. We show that dark-sector interactions can substantially weaken this conclusion. In particular, eV-scale fermions can form bound structures with the characteristic sizes and densities of dwarf galaxies, thereby circumventing the conventional Tremaine-Gunn mass constraint. Within these objects, the degeneracy pressure is balanced by an attractive finite-range scalar Yukawa force that is significantly stronger than gravity. The interaction acts on dwarf-galaxy scales while remaining screened on larger scales. We derive the evolution of the resulting dark-matter fluid and identify a viable cosmological history in which structures first form gravitationally, with the new interaction becoming active only at late times. As a proof of concept, we construct a model in which a single late-time phase transition generates the required dynamics. These results provide a new route to exceptionally light fermionic dark matter and demonstrate that dark-sector interactions can qualitatively alter conventional phase-space limits.

</details>


### [6] [A PQ-Symmetric High-Scale SUSY Interpretation of the LZ High-Energy Recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01892)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出LZ探测器报告的248 keV核反冲类事件可解释为1.08 TeV热Higgsino暗物质近阈值上散射。0.3-0.35 MeV的中性Higgsino劈裂使固定弱相互作用截面接近LZ观测区间，并暗示电弱规范玻色子质量在O(10^7) GeV量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ暗物质实验探测到一个248 keV的核反冲类事件，可能是暗物质直接探测的信号，本文旨在为该候选事件提供一个粒子物理理论解释。

Method: 将事件解释为1.08 TeV热Higgsino暗物质在阈值附近的相干弹性散射；通过调节中性Higgsino的质量劈裂（~0.3-0.35 MeV）使其固定弱相互作用截面与LZ观测一致，并在重力介导高标度超对称与Kim-Nilles Peccei-Quinn扇区框架下构建自洽的理论谱。

Result: 该谱与高标度超对称相容，预测较低的大统一（GUT）标度但质子衰变受高SUSY标度抑制，同时解决CP、味道、gravitino和moduli问题；PQ对称性降低Higgsino质量并提供QCD轴子解决强CP问题；若轴子与Higgsino构成混合暗物质，可放宽依赖于晕轮廓的H.E.S.S.限制。

Conclusion: 该方案为LZ的候选事件提供了一致的高标度超对称解释，并预言未来质子衰变实验与轴子暗物质相关探测可对其进行检验。

Abstract: The 248-keV nuclear-recoil-like event reported by LZ can be interpreted as near-threshold upscattering of 1.08-TeV thermal Higgsino dark matter. A neutral-Higgsino splitting of order $0.3$--$0.35$ MeV places the fixed weak-interaction cross section close to the observed LZ interval and implies electroweak gauginos at ${O}(10^7)$ GeV. This spectrum is compatible with gravity-mediated high-scale supersymmetry and a Kim--Nilles Peccei--Quinn sector. Although the GUT scale is predicted to be lower than the conventional one, proton decay is suppressed due to the high SUSY scale which also solves the conventional CP, flavor, gravitino and moduli problems. Indeed, proton decay may be probed in the future. The PQ symmetry lowers the Higgsino mass below the SUSY scale and also yields a QCD axion that solves the strong-$CP$ problem. If the axion and Higgsino constitute mixed dark matter, the halo-profile-dependent H.E.S.S. limit can be relaxed.

</details>


### [7] [Generalized Foldy-Wouthuysen approach for the derivation of non-relativistic effective field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01980)
*Tobias Asano,Fabio Di Pumpo,Enno Giese,Motoaki Bamba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过先构建相对论性有效场论（EFT），再基于扩展的Foldy-Wouthuysen变换进行广义非相对论（NR）约化，从而获得非相对论EFT的系统方法。该方法使得隐藏洛伦兹不变性直接作为构造的结果自然涌现，适用于包括费米子系统、非惯性时空以及超越标准模型扩展（如轴子耦合）等更复杂的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论有效场论（如NRQED、NRQCD）的构造依赖于隐藏洛伦兹不变性对匹配系数的约束，但这一约束的推导在涉及超越标准模型的物理或非惯性时空背景时变得极其复杂，因为其隐藏对称结构不再透明，需要一种系统化的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者提出一个两步构造策略：首先构建相对论性EFT，然后基于扩展的Foldy-Wouthuysen变换执行广义的非相对论约化。该变换以一种保持对称结构的方式将理论降至NR极限，使得隐藏洛伦兹不变性不是人为施加的约束，而是从构造流程中自然导出的结果。作者以量子色-电动力学EFT（包含电磁与强相互作用）为例验证该方法，并进一步将其应用于含赝标量轴子场的简化模型中导出NR极限。

Result: 该方法成功将量子色-电动力学EFT约化到已确立的NRQCD和NRQED的拉格朗日量，且隐藏洛伦兹不变性由构造直接涌现。该方法的通用性允许将其推广到非惯性时空中的狄拉克场以及涉及超越标准模型物理的扩展理论，文中通过轴子耦合模型进行了示例演示。

Abstract: Effective field theories (EFTs) are a powerful framework for performing high-precision calculations at reduced complexity compared to their fundamental counterparts. A particularly important class of EFTs arises in the non-relativistic (NR) regime. Their construction relies on a different realization of the underlying symmetries, since Lorentz invariance is no longer manifest in covariant form in the NR regime. This behavior imposes a link between certain matching coefficients, and therefore additional constraints, commonly referred to as hidden Lorentz invariance. These constraints are established in quantum field theories on inertial flat spacetime, such as NR quantum electrodynamics. However, deriving these constraints becomes considerably more involved for theories involving physics beyond the Standard Model or formulated in non-inertial spacetime backgrounds, where the hidden symmetry structure is less transparent. In this work, we present an approach to obtain the NR EFT by first constructing a relativistic EFT and then performing a generalized NR reduction based on an extended Foldy-Wouthuysen transformation. We illustrate this method by a quantum chromo-electrodynamics EFT for inertial flat spacetime, describing both electromagnetic and strong interactions, and show how it reduces to the established Lagrangian of NR quantum chromodynamics and electrodynamics. The hidden Lorentz invariance emerges as a direct consequence of the construction. This approach provides a route to obtain the NR limits of more complex theories, \eg Dirac fields in non-inertial spacetime or extensions involving physics beyond the Standard Model. As an example, we apply the method to add the coupling of a pseudoscalar axion field in a simplified model and derive its NR limit.

Overall, combining direct detection, relic density, and indirect detection significantly restricts the viable interpretations of the LZ event and provides concrete targets for future searches.

Our results support the possibility that the minimization of entanglement provides a common information-theoretic principle underlying the suppression of BSM phenomena relevant to symmetry-violating interactions.

Abstract: We comment on the recent work [Eur. Phys. J. C 86, 988 (2026)], which investigates quantum resources in polarized hyperon--antihyperon systems using correlated dephasing channels with memory. Although the use of experimentally reconstructed spin-density matrices to characterize quantum correlations in hyperon production may be a good motivation, we argue that the interpretation of the hyperon--antihyperon pair as an open quantum system undergoing correlated environmental decoherence is not physically established. In particular, no physical environment or system--environment interaction responsible for the assumed correlated dephasing channel is identified, and hadronization cannot simply be interpreted as such an environmental dephasing process. Consequently, the claimed non-Markovianity, information backflow, and memory-assisted protection of quantum resources should be regarded as properties of a phenomenological channel rather than established physical effects in hyperon--antihyperon production. We also identify several technical and conceptual issues concerning the dephasing dynamics, entanglement quantification, basis dependence of coherence, and the claimed hierarchy of quantum resources. These issues call for a substantial revision of the physical interpretation and quantitative conclusions of the work. We hope that the present Comment will help clarify the distinction between phenomenological quantum-channel modeling and physically established dynamical mechanisms in high-energy particle systems, and thereby provide useful guidance for future studies and help avoid similar conceptual and technical issues.

Recent work by King et al. argued that the classical simulation of such experiments would require exponential computational resources for classical methods such as tensor networks and neural quantum states. Here, we numerically simulate the D-Wave spin-glass annealing protocol with time-dependent variational Monte Carlo (t-VMC) using a correlator state tailored to spin-glass dynamics. For the two-dimensional cylinder, three-dimensional dimer, diamond, and biclique instances considered, at annealing times of 7 and 20 ns, we show that systematically increasing the variational ansatz size enables t-VMC to approximate the final two-spin correlation errors of the quantum processing unit (QPU). We also perform an accurate large-scale simulation of a challenging biclique instance for which no other variational method is known to work, and we find close agreement with the quantum annealer. Through ablation studies, we identify poor Markov-chain mixing, high-variance local-energy estimators, and stochastic Runge-Kutta error estimates as the principal numerical failure modes. We address these numerical issues by using parallel tempering, blurred sampling and an importance-weighted differential equation solver, thereby clarifying the numerical requirements for stable, large-scale t-VMC simulations. Our results extend the frontier of classical simulation while providing a realistic assessment of the computational costs of simulating quantum dynamics at this scale.

Unlike classical dynamics, where the two wells are disconnected, in the quantum case, quantum tunneling enables eigenstates to delocalize into the other well, creating nonzero overlaps between adjacent eigenstates that introduce low-frequency components into the evolution of the mean particle position. By comparing quantum and classical dynamics of particles in one-dimensional double-well potentials, we demonstrate that this interference is uniquely quantum and can be identified through the oscillation of the average position alone. We further relate the observed spectral peaks to the structure of the energy eigenvalues. Furthermore, we construct several gauge-invariant observables from the $\mathcal{N}$-bein and its derivatives to highlight the utility of the new tensors. Finally, to illustrate the convenience and applications of this formalism, we apply the theoretical framework to a system of coupled harmonic oscillators immersed in an electric field. Abstract: Many quantum algorithms for classically difficult optimization tasks must return high-quality bitstrings from finitely many circuit executions, whereas most quantum error-mitigation methods target expectation values. Each component of the measured probability mass is called a source and we assume that each center is associated with one source. We identify dominance-at every coordinate, more than half of a retained region's probability mass comes from one source and agrees with its center-as a sufficient condition under which majority voting recovers that center with exponentially decreasing error probability. We show that nearest-center assignment, as used in clustering algorithms such as the $k$-modes algorithm, can fail to produce dominated regions even when the true centers are known. This failure motivates responsibility thresholding and a local dominance screen, whose combination we call dominance-aware (DA) refinement. Synthetic and simulated MaxCut-QAOA experiments show that DA refinement favors precision, while $k$-modes with DA refinement improves overall center recovery. All procedures are classical post-processing and require no additional quantum-circuit executions.

Abstract: A departure of a mixed quantum state from a local Gibbs description is generally invisible to local observables but can be detected by the conditional mutual information (CMI). Here we study the relationship between the renormalization group (RG) and CMI, and in particular, how RG constrains CMI. We first show that the CMI between nonadjacent regions $A$ and $C$, conditioned on the buffer region $B$, is UV-finite whenever the state admits a locally reversible RG with a fixed on-site Hilbert space dimension. We then study two hierarchical models that have long-range CMI and yet admit a simple RG description. Abstract: Distributed quantum sensing requires spatially separated probes to acquire local parameters while maintaining compatibility with network-level quantum information processing. We develop an entanglement-assisted stabilizer framework based on the extended structure of entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting (EAQEC) codes, in which the remote halves of pre-shared ebits are used directly as local phase probes while the joint state simultaneously carries an encoded logical subsystem. Each remote probe acquires a local Z-axis phase and subsequently returns through an X-type noise channel. Within the extended EAQEC stabilizer structure, the stabilizer containing $X_{B_j}$ provides phase-dependent measurement statistics, whereas its partner containing $Z_{B_j}$ records the corresponding return-error syndrome. A graph-code formulation is introduced to make this structure explicit, together with an illustrative [[5,1,3;2]] construction. We further show that, conditioned on the joint sensing-and-syndrome measurement record, the post-sensing state differs from the original encoded state only by a known Pauli transformation, so that the logical information remains available for subsequent encoded operations. For the local-phase model considered here, the stabilizer readout attains the available quantum Fisher information, while the finite-shot estimator approaches the corresponding $1/\sqrt{M}$ scaling as the number of repetitions increases. The framework therefore provides a common entanglement-assisted stabilizer structure for distributed local-phase sensing, restricted return-error identification, and post-sensing logical-state retention, without relying on an intrinsic metrological enhancement from EAQEC itself.

Execution on IBM processors (ibm_cleveland, ibm_miami) successfully recovered native-like structures with a best RMSD of 1.758 Å. The custom energy function performed best overall, though energy-ranking imbalances persisted across sampled landscapes for all functions.

Despite current limits like computational overhead and energy function sensitivity, it establishes a scalable foundation for hybrid quantum biophysics.

Abstract: Semidefinite programming plays a key role in optimization, with broad impact across control theory, machine learning, and combinatorial optimization. Implementing and measuring the method end to end surfaces several effects not visible from theoretical analyses alone: where memory is actually consumed, how the padding required to fit a matrix onto a quantum register can mislead the variational optimizer, and why the candidate matrices prove dense in the Pauli basis, reducing the operator-derived ansatz to full entanglement. Abstract: Quantum generative models offer a promising framework for exploring whether quantum computation can enhance generative machine learning. In contrast, ytterbium ions exhibit zero-first-order Zeeman transitions and large hyperfine splittings at zero magnetic field (when doped into inorganic crystals). We further establish strong spin-microwave coupling from avoided crossing measurements. With a strong microwave drive to saturate the spin transition, we estimate the spin population pumped into the excited state, close to the simulated value. Applying the framework to calibration data from three distinct detector architectures, we demonstrate quantitative comparison across photon-number regimes relevant to both discrete-variable and continuous-variable photonic quantum computing. Abstract: We present a scheme to efficiently simulate nonreciprocal many-body spin models using a non-Markovian open system. Our scheme utilizes a single quantum emitter coupled to a waveguide mode that is fed back to the same emitter after a time delay. With this coherent delayed feedback, an effective nonreciprocal interaction can be engineered, wherein the emitter at earlier times affects itself at later times, creating a scalable spin chain. We show that all the steady-state quantities of such spin models can be accessed through measurement of the emitter and the output field. Furthermore, using classical feedback that resets the emitter, quench dynamics from arbitrary product states can be simulated. We demonstrate striking features of nonreciprocal models, such as the Liouvillian skin effect, anomalous relaxation dynamics, and quasi-long-range order of output photons. Finally, we show that the proposal is amenable to experimental realization and robust to imperfections. Our work establishes a feasible way to scale up a nonreciprocal many-body system, and provides a new route towards generation of exotic states of light.

Abstract: Entanglement distribution through optical fibers is essential for quantum communication networks; however, fiber transmission can alter photon polarization and modify the observed correlations of polarization-entangled states, necessitating polarization compensation to recover the desired entangled state in the measurement basis. Here, we experimentally evaluate two passive polarization compensation techniques: a free-space Quarter-Half-Quarter (QHQ) waveplate configuration and an in-fiber three-paddle Fiber Polarization Controller (FPC). The polarization transformation along each downconverted-photon path is independently compensated through a systematic path-by-path optimization procedure. Using both techniques, we recover high-quality polarization correlations and entanglement, with visibilities exceeding $93\%$ in three mutually unbiased bases and fidelities above $94.5\%$. The results demonstrate comparable restoration using free-space waveplate-based and fiber-based control, establishing a systematic framework for laboratory and short-reach quantum communication links.

Abstract: Logical fusions are important for a number of tasks in quantum information, such as quantum error correction and quantum repeaters. In the photonic setting one must contend with the fact that physical fusions are probabilistic (i.e.~the associated qubits are measured in product bases), which---depending on the failures and the associated bases---can lead to a failure on the logical level. The choice of failure basis of each qubit is known as a fusion strategy, and finding good fusion strategies is important for optimizing performance of fusion-based quantum computation. Here we provide a complete characterization when $k=1$ qubits are encoded, and in particular characterize those codes and fusion strategies such that all but one physical fusion can fail, i.e.~\emph{perfect fusion strategies}. In doing so, we recover previously known perfect fusion strategies, and find perfect fusion strategies for quantum parity-check codes, answering an open question. We furthermore show that perfect fusion strategies are generic: random $[[n, 1, d]]$ graph codes admit a perfect fusion strategy with probability exponentially close to $1$. Additionally we motivate the study of a new graph parameter, namely the maximum degree of a graph at a given vertex taken over all LC-equivalent graphs, by giving a new operationally meaningful interpretation of it.

</details>

Abstract: We present a combinatorial optimization problem for the strategic deployment of UAVs equipped with 5G antennas to assist rescue operations in regions hit by natural disasters. Our goal is to optimize the placement of UAVs to provide coverage in flying ad-hoc networks among given candidate sites. Our formulation aims to maximize signal coverage and minimize interference while ensuring network connectivity. To mitigate interference effects, we incorporate the use of multiple frequencies. We formulate this problem as an integer quadratic program (IQP). We present numerical solutions obtained via the CPLEX solver and conduct a preliminary analysis of the problem's scalability in realistic network configurations. Our findings reveal a significant exponential increase in Time-to-Solution (TTS) as the number of sites grows, which poses a critical challenge in urgent, time-sensitive scenarios. To address this issue, approximate suboptimal solutions can be produced by enforcing a time limit on the solver. Although these solutions are not optimal, they preserve connectivity in most cases, providing a practical trade-off between solution quality and computational times that remain within feasible limits for real-time UAV redeployment. Recognizing the limitations of classical solvers in these contexts, we explore quantum computing as a promising alternative. Specifically, we reformulate the problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem, suitable for most quantum algorithms. Through high-performance computing emulation, we show that the quantum adiabatic algorithm (QAA) can accurately solve small-scale instances, paving the way for future application of quantum computing to large-scale, time-critical optimization problems in disaster response.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, Thouless pumping in a one-dimensional semi-infinite Fock-state lattice is investigated. A distinctive feature of such lattices is the intrinsic $\sqrt{n}$-dependent coupling arising from the bosonic mode, which leads to spatially nonuniform hopping amplitudes. In the dimer limit, the topological invariants and the quantized transport dynamics in the Fock-state basis are numerically evaluated and analyzed. By introducing an additional inter-cell coupling and applying a squeezing transformation, the framework is then extended to Thouless pumping in the squeezed Fock-state basis, where a topologically protected scheme for preparing superpositions of squeezed Fock states is proposed. This study establishes Thouless pumping in Fock-state lattices as a useful tool for quantum state engineering, shifting the focus from observing topological transport to harnessing it for the preparation of non-classical states of the bosonic mode.

Abstract: Long-lived storage of single photons under the form of atomic excitations is at the foundation of long-distance entanglement distribution in quantum networks. To mitigate decoherence effects induced by the environment, rephasing of the hyperfine coherences using microwave pulses have been implemented in a variety of single-emitter and ensemble-based solid-state systems. However, the demonstration of storage of single photons in an absorptive quantum memory including such spin rephasing mechanism remains elusive. In this work, we show non-classical storage of telecom-heralded single photons in a Pr$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ rare-earth ion doped crystal quantum memory using the atomic frequency comb (AFC) spin-wave protocol combined with a XY4 spin rephasing sequence. Long-lived AFC photon echoes are first observed in the classical regime for storage times of up to approximately 3 ms. We then demonstrate non-classical correlations between heralding photons and stored signal photons generated by a cavity-enhanced parametric photon-pair source for storage times of up to 180 $μ$s and with measured cross-correlation values as high as 4.6(4). Together with the capacity of Pr$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ QMs to support highly efficient and multiplexed storage, this result represents a significant step towards scalable long-distance quantum repeater links.

Abstract: A key task in many quantum-computing applications, e.g., quantum simulation and quantum state tomography (QST), is to partition an arbitrary set of operators into mutually commuting subsets for efficient measurements. We validate our framework across multi-qubit (up to five qubits), multi-qutrit (up to three qutrits), and hybrid qubit-qutrit systems. Our results show that heuristic GC methods substantially reduce the number of required measurement settings for QST and enable priority-based scheduling that maximizes the information gain per experiment. The optimization converges within minutes on a student-grade laptop, providing speedups of several orders of magnitude over brute-force methods already for these relatively small quantum systems. This demonstrates the potential of GC heuristics as a scalable and practical tool for characterization of noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices. Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires not only reliable logical qubit storage, but also the ability to perform high-fidelity logical operations between error-corrected qubits at scale. While much of the existing literature focuses on optimizing syndrome extraction for a single logical qubit, the co-design of physical architectures that support both robust error correction and efficient logical computation remains an open challenge. In this work, we propose a multi-qubit spin-qubit shuttling bus architecture that addresses both requirements simultaneously. The architecture optimizes the physical qubit layout for syndrome extraction and supports transversal two-qubit logical gates between an arbitrary number of logical qubits, achieving all-to-all logical connectivity through coherent spin shuttling. We further propose an ancilla-sharing scheme that encodes multiple logical qubits within a single logical element, compressing the physical footprint of the processor and improving long-range gate fidelity. Extending the architecture from a one-dimensional bus to a two-dimensional grid of shuttling tracks reduces the inter-qubit distance, yielding consistent improvements in logical error. Finally, we apply the Quantum Reverse Mapping methodology at the logical level to optimize the layout of a \textit{15-to-1} magic state distillation circuit, demonstrating how the transversal capabilities of the proposed architecture can be leveraged for universal fault-tolerant computation. Taken together, these results establish a principled co-design framework that bridges the physical, error-correction, and logical computation layers of the quantum stack, and demonstrate that spin-qubit shuttling architectures are a viable and flexible substrate for scalable fault-tolerant quantum computation.

Abstract: We initiate the time-bounded meta-complexity program for quantum cryptography. Recent work characterizes one-way puzzles, the minimal search primitive of quantum cryptography without one-way functions, by the average-case hardness of approximating the plain, uncomputable Kolmogorov complexity over quantumly samplable distributions; the classical program of Liu and Pass, by contrast, lives at polynomial time bounds. We define a probabilistic time-bounded quantum program complexity pKq^t for classical strings and prove two unconditional theorems. First, a quantum coding theorem: any string output by a quantum polynomial-time sampler with probability delta admits a description of the information-theoretically optimal length log(1/delta) plus logarithmic terms, decodable by a quantum machine in time O(sqrt(1/delta)) times a polynomial, via amplitude amplification over the coherently executed sampler. Second, an exact characterization at subexponential time: one-way puzzles exist if and only if the gap problem for pKq at time bound 2^(n/2) poly(n) is weakly quantum-average-hard, refining the plain-complexity characterizations. We then isolate the polynomial-time coding theorem as the single load-bearing open conjecture of the program, prove that it implies the full polynomial-time characterization, analyze why the classical derandomization proof resists quantization, and formulate a relativized barrier conjecture delimiting string-valued meta-complexity at one-way puzzles. Conjectures are labeled as such throughout.

Abstract: Non-adaptive quantum amplitude estimation (QAE) fixes its Grover depths in advance, so every circuit can run in parallel, but it has so far needed more queries than the best adaptive methods. We show that most of this gap comes from two conventional choices: subspace-based post-processing and power-of-two depth ladders. We replace the first by the exact maximum-likelihood estimate, one matrix multiplication per batch of estimates, and the second by a geometric ladder with ratio $r \approx 1.45$. The result is a fully parallel, deterministic-schedule estimator with total query complexity $2.8$-$3.1/\varepsilon$ at 95% confidence for target errors from $3.5\times 10^{-3}$ to $10^{-6}$. This matches the average-case complexity of chebAE, the best benchmarked adaptive method, within statistical uncertainty (with the lower point estimate at every scale tested), beats its maximum-observed complexity by $1.6\times$, and needs a maximum sequential depth of only $0.21/\varepsilon$ against chebAE's $2.9/\varepsilon$. Relative to csAE, the best non-adaptive benchmark, the constants improve by 30-35% at 95% and $1.5$-$1.7\times$ at 99% confidence. The optimal ratio has a simple origin. Doubling is the fastest depth growth at which the data can still tell neighboring candidate values apart, so power-of-two ladders sit at the edge of confusion and must buy reliability with extra shots; a slightly denser ladder checks every scale redundantly. An error-probability analysis reproduces the measured failure rates and locates the optimum. The likelihood formulation extends directly to noise-aware estimation, and uniformly scaling the capped ladder covers the depth-limited regime, realizing the optimal trade-off $M N_{\mathrm{tot}} \approx (0.4$-$0.6)/\varepsilon^2$ within $\sim 1.1\times$ of the schedule's Cramér-Rao limit.

Abstract: Claims about a deployed quantum machine-learning classifier can fail when target data shift or when finite-shot quantum evaluation randomizes the model itself. Abstract: The demonstration that enhanced yellow fluorescent protein hosts an optically addressable spin-1 qubit in its metastable triplet state raises the prospect of genetically encoded quantum sensing at molecular length scales.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文将She和Yuen（ITCS '23）的酉不变多项式方法与一个新构造相结合：利用对称与反对称子空间投影算子来构造量子预言机。通过该构造，将QMA下界归约到布尔函数OR的近似度（approximate degree）问题。

Result: 找到了一个量子预言机，相对于该预言机QMA ≠ QMA(2)。由此推论，对于所有ε+δ<1，任何(ε,δ)-去纠缠器所需的输入规模随输出量子比特数呈指数增长，从而解决了Watrous的no-disentanglers猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果在量子预言机模型下分离了QMA与QMA(2)，证实了多证明者量子验算系统中非纠缠条件的本质困难性，即两个非纠缠证明者的验证能力在相对化意义下严格强于单个证明者。这为最终证明无相对化分离提供了重要证据和技术基础。

Abstract: We find a quantum oracle relative to which $\mathsf{QMA} \neq \mathsf{QMA}(2)$. As a consequence, we resolve the no-disentanglers conjecture of Watrous: for every $ε+δ<1$, any $(ε,δ)$-disentangler requires input size exponential in the number of output qubits. Our proof combines the unitarily invariant polynomial method of She and Yuen (ITCS '23) with a new construction based on the symmetric and antisymmetric subspace projectors, reducing the $\mathsf{QMA}$ lower bound to the approximate degree of $\mathrm{OR}$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找高于四维时流形上不存在辛结构的障碍。高维缺少类似四维Seiberg–Witten理论那样能产生不变量并给出障碍的工具，因此需要构造一个在四维退化为Seiberg–Witten方程的高维推广系统。

Method: 在几乎Hermitian流形上选择spin^C结构，构造一个椭圆方程组（高维Seiberg–Witten型方程）；分析其Fredholm指标；在几乎Kähler流形上通过特定的扰动构造典范解并验证横截性；在Kähler情形通过自然ansatz将方程约化为涡旋方程进行研究。

Result: 1) 方程组在典范spin^C结构下指标为零；2) 在几乎Kähler流形上，扰动参数足够大时典范解是横截的；3) 六维时在特定假设下典范解唯一；4) Kähler流形上方程可约化为涡旋方程。

Abstract: Motivated by the problem of finding obstructions to the existence of symplectic structures in dimensions higher than four, we introduce an elliptic system of equations on almost Hermitian manifolds that reduces to the Seiberg--Witten equations with Taubes' perturbation in dimension four. To define the equations, one needs to choose a spin$^{\mathbb C}$ structure on the manifold. We prove that the system has index zero for the canonical spin$^{\mathbb C}$ structure. Moreover, on almost Kähler (symplectic) manifolds, we construct a canonical solution and prove its transversality with large values of the perturbation parameter, while in dimension six we obtain uniqueness of the canonical solution under a certain assumption. We also show that on Kähler manifolds the equations reduce, under a natural ansatz, to the vortex equations. These results provide evidence toward a possible higher dimensional gauge theory capable of producing invariants of almost complex structures and ultimately, obstructions to compatible symplectic forms.

Abstract: We study locally conformally flat (LCF) Riemannian manifolds with nonnegative or positive scalar curvature (PSC), using the conformal boundary of the developing image. For closed oriented LCF $n$-manifolds with PSC and infinite fundamental group, $n\ge5$, we bound the macroscopic dimension of their Riemannian universal covers by $\lfloor(n-1)/2\rfloor$, establish the existence of a nontrivial homotopy group above the middle dimension, and, under mild additional hypotheses, bound the Hausdorff dimension of the limit sets of their Kleinian groups in the interval $(1,(n-2)/2)$.

We prove that the space of LCF metrics with PSC and its moduli space are contractible in dimension three for finite fundamental group and for $S^2\times S^1$, and that the moduli space is empty or contractible for smooth manifolds homeomorphic to spherical space forms but not diffeomorphic to $S^n$ in dimensions $n\ge4$. We also show that, in dimensions $n\ge4$, the Euclidean conclusion can fail when neither of the two additional topological hypotheses is assumed: we construct complete contractible examples of PSC that are not homeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^n$.

Abstract: We study formal deformations of the natural $\osp(n|2)$-action, $n\geq 3$, on the superspace $\Sc^n_d=\bigoplus_{k\geq 0}\Fc^n_{d-\frac{k}{2}}$ of symbols of linear differential operators on weighted densities over $\R^{1|n}$. Starting from the first cohomology space computed in \cite{10}, we compute the cup-product $\Hd^1\vee \Hd^1\to \Hd^2$ which carries the quadratic obstructions. The answer is governed by the $\osp(n|2)$-invariant operators $A_k=η_1\cdotsη_n\partial_x^{k-1}$: the two cocycles $h_k$ and $\widetilde{h}_k$ spanning the off-diagonal part of $\Hd^1$ are exactly the two derivatives of the coboundary of $A_k$ with respect to the two weights. If $2d\notin\N$ the space $\Hd^1\vee\Hd^1$ is identically zero, so every infinitesimal deformation is integrable. If $2d=m\in\N$ we obtain exactly $m$ quadratic integrability conditions, $τ_{2-n-k}(t_k-\widetilde{t}_k)+τ_k\widetilde{t}_k=0$, $1\leq k\leq m$, and we prove that they are also sufficient: no condition of order $\geq 3$ occurs and the versal deformation is of degree one in the parameters. On a general equiregular manifold, convergence for every smooth test function is equivalent to convergence of the rescaled horizontal first moments of metric balls in first-kind privileged coordinates. This criterion is independent of $μ$; when it holds, the principal symbol is determined by the normalized second-moment tensor of the tangent unit ball, and the drift satisfies an explicit change-of-measure formula. Starting from one singular orbit, we follow the Einstein solutions only to a canonical hypersurface and measure there how far they are from closing smoothly. When the singular-orbit scale becomes small, this closing problem is governed by a Ricci-flat limiting solution. The linearisattion about its limiting cone has an oscillatory mode. As the scale shrinks, this oscillation repeatedly changes the sign of the closing error, producing infinitely many parameter values for which the metric closes. The closing scales satisfy an asymptotic logarithmic quantization law, with successive ratio tending to \(e^{-2π/\sqrt{15}}\). We also determine the geometry of the resulting sequence. Away from the two singular orbits the metrics converge to the singular sine cone over the non-normal Einstein metric on \(SU(3)/T^2\), while after rescaling by the square of the singular-orbit scale at either end they converge to the same complete Ricci-flat threshold metric.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在曲率下限条件下Laplace特征值的最优化问题，特别是理解高斯曲率至少为1的闭曲面中第k个特征值最小化元的几何性质，以及其极限行为（是否可能塌缩）。

Method: 利用一维CD(1,2)空间的Neumann特征值比较定理，与Legendre模型进行对比；在密度满足凸性条件下建立尖锐不等式并证明Obata型刚性；结合变分方法证明光滑最小化元的圆球面性质。

Result: 建立了CD(1,2)空间上一维Neumann特征值的Legendre模型尖锐比较定理及Obata型刚性；证明了曲率≥1闭曲面上的第k个特征值最小化元在测度Gromov-Hausdorff完备化中不可塌缩；光滑最小化元必为圆球面。

Conclusion: 高斯曲率至少为1的闭曲面Laplace特征值最小化问题具有确定的极限结构，最小化元不能退化塌缩，且在光滑情形下唯一地被圆球面实现。

Abstract: We prove a sharp comparison, with Obata-type rigidity, for all Neumann eigenvalues of one-dimensional $\mathrm{CD}(1,2)$ spaces against the Legendre model, under a convexity condition on the density. It follows that minimizers of the $k$-th Laplace eigenvalue among closed surfaces of Gaussian curvature at least $1$ cannot collapse in the measured Gromov-Hausdorff completion, for every $k\ge2$. We also give a variational proof that smooth minimizers are round.

</details>


### [101] [The universal moduli space of non-degenerate $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ harmonic spinors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02848)
*Siqi He,Gregory J. Parker,Thomas Walpuski*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为无退化Z/2Z调和旋量的万有模空间赋予了驯服Fréchet流形结构，并证明投影到参数空间的映射是一致Fredholm的，从而统一恢复了Donaldson、Parker和Takahashi的早期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和旋量模空间在微分几何与规范理论中具有重要地位，但以往关于其结构的研究是分裂的。作者希望建立一个统一的框架，涵盖并恢复Donaldson、Parker和Takahashi等人的独立结果，为Z/2Z调和旋量万有模空间提供严格的分析基础。

Method: 利用驯服Fréchet流形的理论框架，将无退化Z/2Z调和旋量的万有模空间构造为适当的流形，并通过分析纤维化映射，证明该投影算子满足一致Fredholm性质。该方法将几何PDE问题转化到无穷维流形与Fredholm理论的统一框架中处理。

Result: 成功证明了无退化Z/2Z调和旋量的万有模空间具有驯服Fréchet流形结构，且投影到参数空间的映射是一致Fredholm的，并在此统一框架中恢复了Donaldson、Parker、Takahashi等人的早期独立结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Z/2Z调和旋量模空间理论的统一分析基础，为后续研究其全局几何与拓扑性质提供了可靠的无穷维流形结构保证，同时展示了驯服Fréchet流形方法在几何分析中的适用性。

Abstract: This article equips the universal moduli spaces of non-degenerate $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ harmonic spinors with the structure of a tame Fréchet manifold and proves that the projection map to the parameter space is uniformly Fredholm. This recovers earlier work of Donaldson, Parker, and Takahashi in a unified framework.

</details>


### [102] [Finite-Time Singularities of the Kähler--Ricci Flow on Fano Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02878)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Fano丛上的未归一化Kähler-Ricci流在有限时间内的塌缩奇性。作者证明了最大分裂定理：在每个固定极限点处的切空间全局分裂为(C^m,g_E,J_0)×(Z',d',J')。对于纤维维数为1的情形，证明了全曲率张量的全局Type-I界，纤维的内蕴直径与环境直径均一致地与√(T-t)同阶，且每个极限点处的切流都是标准收缩圆柱C^m×P^1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究解析极小模型纲领中出现的Fano丛上Kähler-Ricci流在塌缩过程中的有限时间奇性结构，旨在理解流在有限时间内塌缩的几何行为及极限流形的结构。

Method: 通过对Fano丛X^n→Y^m上的未归一化Kähler-Ricci流进行奇性分析，建立最大分裂定理；在纤维维数为1时利用曲率估计推导Type-I全局界，并通过直径估计及切流分析揭示塌缩几何形态。

Result: 证明了任意固定极限点处的切空间全局分裂为欧氏子流形与奇性子流形的乘积；纤维维数为1时，全曲率张量满足全局Type-I界，纤维的内蕴与环境直径均与√(T-t)可比，且所有切流均为标准收缩圆柱C^m×P^1。

Conclusion: 该结果为Fano丛上Kähler-Ricci流塌缩奇性的结构提供了精确刻画，将塌缩行为归结为纤维维数1情形的标准收缩圆柱模型，深化了对解析极小模型纲领中塌缩机制的理解。

Abstract: We study collapsing finite-time singularities of the unnormalized Kähler--Ricci flow on Fano bundles arising in the analytic minimal model program. For a Fano bundle $X^n\rightarrow Y^m$, we prove a maximal splitting theorem by the Kähler-Ricci flow that every tangent space at a fixed limiting point splits globally as \((\C^m,g_{\rm E},J_0)\times(Z',d',J')\). If the fibre has complex dimension one, we prove a global Type-I bound for the full curvature tensor and show that the ambient and intrinsic diameters of every fibre are uniformly comparable to \(\sqrt{T-t}\). Furthermore, every tangent flow at any fixed limiting point is the round shrinking cylinder \(\C^m\times\PP^1\).

</details>

### [103] [Unfolded Krylov complexity: universal chaotic dynamics without false positives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02228)
*Johanna Erdmenger,Kyoung-Bum Huh,Hyun-Sik Jeong,Juan F. Pedraza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出在构建Krylov态复杂度（spread complexity）之前对谱进行"unfolding"（展开/解折叠）处理，以去除非普遍的光滑态密度，从而消除可积系统中虚假的混沌信号峰，使展开后的Krylov复杂度成为更可靠的量子混沌诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov态复杂度（spread complexity）常被用来诊断量子混沌，其特征性的增长、峰值和弛豫被视为混沌的签名。但已有研究表明该判据存在误报：具有鞍点主导的可积系统即使缺乏随机矩阵能级关联，也可能表现出显著的峰值。因此需要一种方法区分真正的多体量子纠缠（scrambling）与谱的动力学效应。

Method: 本文提出在构造Krylov动力学之前对谱进行unfolding（展开）处理。通过去除非普遍的光滑态密度同时保留微观谱关联，展开抑制了可积系统中的虚假峰，同时保留混沌系统的普遍谱特征。解析上，作者将Lanczos迭代表述为正交多项式形式，得到具有稳健近对角结构的精确复杂度核，其精细结构反映底层谱关联。对于对数模型，unfolding可精确执行，将谱映射到均匀格点，给出解析的spread complexity，消除误报峰。

Result: 数值与解析证据共同表明：unfolding能有效抑制可积系统中由鞍点导致的虚假峰，同时保留混沌系统Krylov复杂度的普遍谱签名。对数模型中的精确解进一步验证了该方法的有效性，展现了unfolding后复杂度峰与真实谱关联之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 展开后的Krylov复杂度（unfolded Krylov complexity）是更为可靠的量子混沌诊断工具，能够区分真正的多体量子纠缠与谱的动力学效应，消除了此前判据的假阳性问题。

Abstract: A central challenge in diagnosing quantum chaos is to distinguish genuine many-body scrambling from kinematic effects of the spectrum. Krylov state complexity, or spread complexity, has emerged as a powerful diagnostic, with its characteristic growth, peak, and relaxation often taken as signatures of chaos. However, previous work has shown that this criterion can give false positives: saddle-dominated integrable systems may display prominent peaks even without random-matrix level correlations. We argue, based on complementary numerical and analytical evidence, that this ambiguity can be resolved by unfolding the spectrum prior to constructing the ensuing Krylov dynamics. By removing the non-universal smooth density of states while retaining microscopic spectral correlations, unfolding suppresses spurious peaks in integrable systems while preserving the universal spectral signatures of chaotic systems. Analytically, the formulation of the Lanczos iteration in terms of orthogonal polynomials yields an exact complexity kernel with a robust near-diagonal structure whose fine-grained features reflect the underlying spectral correlations. Moreover, for the logarithmic model, unfolding can be performed exactly, mapping the spectrum to a uniform lattice and yielding an analytic spread complexity that removes the false-positive peak. These findings establish unfolded Krylov complexity as a more reliable probe of genuine many-body scrambling.

</details>


### [104] [Holographic algebras at null infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01706)
*Chang-Han Chen,Geoff Penington,Gautam Satishchandran,Elisa Tabor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在渐近平坦时空中构造了与零无穷远处切割相关的可观测量代数。尖锐切割的Bondi质量不是良定义的量子算符（即使在延迟时间中涂抹，其涨落仍发散），因此作者引入有限半径、时间涂抹版本的Bondi质量，并将其与非微扰地并入切割的任意小渐近邻域中的物质和引力子可观测量。在有无黑洞两种情形下均得到满足非平凡嵌套关系的Type III₁ von Neumann代数。在Minkowski时空中重建了与切割相关的类空楔；在稳态黑洞时空中重建了由切割和黑洞双曲面边界区域，构成渐近平坦类比下的纠缠楔。当切割移至过去无穷远处时，无黑洞情形恢复Type I∞代数，黑洞情形恢复Type II∞代数。对有限切割的延时代数，构造了黑洞代数的Type II∞正则化，其重整化von Neumann熵与广义熵一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在渐近平坦时空中建立量子引力意义上的可观测量代数框架，特别是理解与零无穷远相关的量子观测者在Bondi框架下可测量的物理量。传统Bondi质量在量子层面因涨落发散而不良定义，需要寻找合适的替代构造来研究渐近平坦时空中的全息对应和纠缠结构。

Method: 采用代数量子场论方法：1) 将Bondi质量替换为有限半径、延迟时间涂抹版本；2) 在切割附近的小渐近邻域中粘合物质与引力子可观测量；3) 分别分析Minkowski时空和无黑洞/有黑洞时空的von Neumann代数结构；4) 通过取切割至过去无穷远的极限恢复不同代数类型；5) 构造黑洞代数有限切割的Type II∞正则化以计算重整化熵；6) 在Schwarzschild时空中证明分裂性质及Hartle-Hawking权的构造（附录）。

Result: 无黑洞和有黑洞情形均得到Type III₁ von Neumann代数且满足非平凡嵌套关系。Minkowski时空中代数重建了切割相关的类空楔区域；黑洞时空中重建了切割与黑洞双曲面之间的区域（渐近平坦纠缠楔类比）。切割移至过去无穷远极限：无黑洞恢复Type I∞，黑洞恢复Type II∞。附录中证明了Schwarzschild时空中趋向Minkowski真空的量子场满足分裂性质，并构造了模流为Schwarzschild时间演化的忠实、正规、半有限Hartle-Hawking权。

Conclusion: 论文建立了渐近平坦时空中与零无穷远切割相关的量子可观测量代数的完整分类框架。通过在有限半径上对Bondi质量进行时间涂抹解决了其量子涨落发散问题，揭示了渐近平坦时空与AdS/CFT类似的代数结构，并在黑洞情形下建立了与纠缠楔、广义熵的对应关系。附录中的技术结果（分裂性质与Hartle-Hawking权）为Schwarzschild背景下的进一步量子场论分析提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We construct an algebra of observables associated to a cut of null infinity in asymptotically flat spacetimes. The Bondi mass associated with a sharp cut of future null infinity is not a well-defined quantum operator: its fluctuations diverge even after smearing in retarded time. We instead introduce a finite-radius, time-smeared version of the Bondi mass and adjoin this operator to the matter and graviton observables in an arbitrarily small asymptotic neighborhood of the cut. We separately analyze spacetimes with and without black holes and find, in both cases, Type III$_1$ von Neumann algebras that satisfy non-trivial nesting relations. In Minkowski spacetime, the resulting algebra reconstructs the spacelike wedge associated with the cut. In a stationary black hole spacetime, it reconstructs the region bounded by the cut and the black hole bifurcation surface, providing an asymptotically flat analogue of an entanglement wedge. In the limit as the cut is moved to past infinity, we recover a Type I$_{\infty}$ algebra for spacetimes without black holes and a Type II$_{\infty}$ algebra for black hole spacetimes. For algebras at finite cuts of retarded time, we construct a Type II$_\infty$ regularization of the black hole algebra whose renormalized von Neumann entropy agrees with the generalized entropy. In appendices, we prove two technical results about quantum fields, in a Schwarzschild spacetime, that asymptote to the Minkowski vacuum at infinity: a split property for unbounded, spacelike separated regions and the construction of a faithful, normal, and semifinite Hartle-Hawking weight whose modular flow is Schwarzschild time evolution.

</details>


### [105] [On-shell recursion and soft theorems for worldline quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01714)
*Nathan Castet,Vincent F. He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了世界线量子场论（WQFT）中的壳上递归关系，通过推导并利用一个新的软引力子定理（推广了Weinberg和前次软定理，包含了世界线软发射的额外贡献），克服了全线复杂动量形变下振幅大-z衰减不佳的问题，得到了任意n-引力子有理振幅可由低点WQFT和纯引力振幅表示的d维递归公式，并验证了二、三引力子振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WQFT是研究经典引力可观测量的有效理论，其中涉及大量引力子振幅的计算。由于全线复杂动量形变下振幅的大-z行为较差（poor large-z falloff），标准的BCFW递归关系无法适用，因此需要寻找新的递归方法来高效计算振幅。

Method: 1) 在所有外部线（包括世界线）上进行复动量形变；2) 推导并利用世界线存在情况下新的软引力子定理，该定理推广了Weinberg软定理和次软定理，新增了来自世界线软发射的贡献；3) 利用该软定理处理振幅在大-z极限下的边界项；4) 建立d维递归公式，将任意n-引力子有理振幅表示为低点WQFT和纯引力振幅的组合；5) 利用软-世界线定理从全引力子振幅获得任意数目外部世界线涨落的振幅。

Result: 得到了适用于WQFT的递归关系，通过显式计算二引力子和三引力子振幅验证了递归公式的正确性，且表明该递归方法足以构造该理论的任何有理振幅（包括带世界线涨落的振幅）。

Conclusion: 该工作为WQFT中引力子振幅的高效计算提供了一套完整的递归方案，拓展了软定理在含世界线框架下的应用，并为进一步计算经典引力可观测量（如散射角和引力波辐射）奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: We derive a set of on-shell recursion relations for amplitudes in worldline quantum field theory (WQFT), an effective theory of gravity coupled to a massive worldline which is relevant for computing classical gravitational observables. We overcome the poor large-$z$ falloff of amplitudes under an all-line complex momentum deformation by deriving and exploiting a new soft-graviton theorem in the presence of a worldline. This theorem generalizes the leading (Weinberg) and subleading soft theorems with additional contributions due to soft emission from the worldline. Our main result is a $d$-dimensional recursion formula, expressing any $n$-graviton rational amplitude in terms of lower-point amplitudes from WQFT and pure gravity. Using the soft-worldline theorem, we can obtain amplitudes with any number of external worldline fluctuations from the corresponding all-graviton amplitudes, making the recursion sufficient to construct any rational amplitude of the theory. We verify our results by explicitly computing two-graviton and three-graviton amplitudes.

</details>


### [106] [Truncated Polyakov bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01732)
*Kaushik Kangshabanik,Apratim Kaviraj,Shailesh Lal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Polyakov bootstrap框架中建立了截断数值方法，利用梯度下降优化来数值求解PB和规则，从而以迭代方式从微扰解出发求解广义自由场谱的任意形变的交叉方程。研究发现幺正形变解与极值CFT谱一致，更重要的是，一般交叉解（即使不满足正定性）可以通过与GFF的光滑连接唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polyakov bootstrap方法是一种用于约束共形场论（CFT）谱的有效框架，但已有的数值求解方法通常依赖于正定性条件。作者希望探索在没有幺正性（即正定性）约束的情况下，是否仍然可以唯一确定交叉方程的解，这需要建立一种新的数值方法来求解PB和规则。

Method: 采用截断数值方法处理Polyakov bootstrap框架，使用梯度下降优化算法来数值求解PB和规则。求解过程从广义自由场（GFF）的微扰解出发，以迭代方式逐步处理GFF谱的任意形变，从而解决交叉方程问题。方法在单相关器和混合相关器两种设置中均进行了验证。

Result: 对于幺正形变，数值解与极值CFT谱相一致。更重要的发现是，即便在没有正定性（幺正性）约束的一般情况下，交叉解仍可通过与GFF的光滑连接实现唯一确定。该方法已在单相关器与混合相关器场景中通过多个实例得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，Polyakov bootstrap方法并不必然依赖幺正性条件，与GFF的光滑连接性可以作为替代的求解唯一性判定准则。这为更广泛的交叉方程解的探索提供了新的途径，扩展了bootstrap方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We set up a truncated numerical approach in the Polyakov bootstrap (PB) framework. We employ a gradient descent optimization to solve PB sum rules numerically, and hence solve crossing for arbitrary deformations of the generalized free field (GFF) spectrum in an iterative way starting from a perturbative solution. For unitary deformations the solutions coincide with extremal CFT spectra. But more interestingly, our findings indicate that a general crossing solution, not necessarily unitary (i.e. without positivity), can be uniquely identified by a smooth connection to GFF. The truncated Polyakov bootstrap approach is established through a number of examples in both single and mixed correlator settings.

</details>


### [107] [Pre-Strings Lectures on Holographic Correlators and Analytic Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01986)
*Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是对Pre-Strings 2026 School五场讲座的扩展笔记，系统地介绍了利用bootstrap策略计算全息关联函数的方法，以4维N=4超Yang-Mills理论的四点函数为例，并讨论了多点函数、量子修正、弦论修正以及带共形缺陷的全息CFT等扩展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年中，bootstrap策略已经深刻改变了全息关联函数的计算方式，并揭示了AdS中散射振幅纲领的新结构。本文旨在为读者提供一个教学性的、系统的综述，整合这些分散在大量文献中的发展，帮助初学者和研究者理解这一快速演进领域的方法论与最新进展。

Method: 文章从CFT基础和AdS微扰论回顾出发，以4维N=4超Yang-Mills理论为范例，详细演示了bootstrap策略在经典IIB超引力对应区间的四点函数计算中的应用。随后讨论多种扩展情景，包括高维关联函数、量子（圈）修正、弦尺度修正，以及将bootstrap方法推广到具有共形缺陷的全息CFT的各种构型。

Result: 文章系统梳理了bootstrap策略的核心思想与操作流程，展示了其在标准全息关联函数计算中的成功应用，并明确了不同扩展方向（多点、量子/弦修正、缺陷构型）各自的适用性、技术挑战和当前进展水平。

Conclusion: bootstrap策略已经成为计算全息关联函数的重要范式工具，其应用场景从最简单的四点函数不断扩展至更复杂的量子与弦论修正以及带缺陷的CFT，预示着AdS散射振幅纲领将持续发展并揭示更深层的结构。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the bootstrap strategy has transformed the computation of holographic correlators and revealed structures suggesting an emerging scattering amplitude program in AdS. These notes, which are an extended version of the five lectures delivered at the Pre-Strings 2026 School, give a pedagogical introduction and synthesis of these developments. We start with a quick reminder of the essentials of CFT and a brief review of AdS perturbation theory. We then demonstrate in detail the bootstrap strategy for computing holographic correlators in the canonical example 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, at the level of four-point functions and in the regime dual to classical Type IIB supergravity. Several extensions, including higher-point functions, quantum corrections and stringy corrections, are discussed at varying levels of detail. We also explain how the bootstrap strategy can be extended to holographic CFTs with conformal defects, covering a diverse array of different setups.

</details>


### [108] [Residues on Permanent Pinches: Finite Integrals and Leading Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02300)
*Dimitri Corradini,Cristian Vergu,Shun-Qing Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从"永久收缩"(permanent pinch)的角度研究红外(IR)发散。作者展示了如何通过消除永久收缩来构造有限的积分线性组合，并发展了一种无需引入正规化器即可计算与方案无关的前导红外发散的方法——在永久收缩簇的去奇异化上积分某些留数形式。该方法适用于无质量积分、混合有质量-无质量积分、具有任意分子的非平面积分以及具有高次(整数)传播子幂的积分。作者将其应用于若干箱型积分的前导奇异项分析，并研究了高圈有限积分实例，特别是为一种非平面两圈五点拓扑提供了有限非消逝积分的一组基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外发散是量子场论中普遍存在的困难，传统方法依赖引入正规化器来处理发散，但正规化方案的依赖性和计算的复杂性构成瓶颈。作者希望从永久收缩的几何视角出发，建立一种无需正规化器、方案无关地计算前导红外发散的框架，从而更简洁而根本性地理解红外发散的结构。

Method: 作者利用永久收缩(pinch)理论来刻画红外发散的结构。核心策略是通过抵消永久收缩构造有限的积分线性组合；进一步在永久收缩簇的去奇异化上积分特定的留数形式，从而无需引入正规化器即可提取方案无关的前导红外发散。该方法适用于一般性的积分类型，包括无质量、混合质量、非平面含任意分子以及高次传播子幂的情形。作者在若干示例(如箱型积分)中验证该方法，并应用于高圈有限的实例。

Result: 作者成功展示了如何在永久收缩视角下形成有限的积分组合，并给出了无需正规化器计算方案无关前导红外发散的具体方法。在具体例子中确定了若干箱型积分的领先奇异项，并在更高圈上提供了非平面两圈五点拓扑上有有限非消逝积分的基底。

Conclusion: 文章表明红外发散可以完全从永久收缩的代数几何结构来理解，所建立的"无正规化器"方法在普适性(涵盖无质量、混合质量、非平面、任意分子及高次幂)上具有明显优势，并为高圈有限积分的研究提供了新的工具和基底构建方案。

Abstract: We study infrared (IR) divergences from the point of view of permanent pinches. We show how canceling permanent pinches allows us to form finite linear combinations of integrals. We further show how to compute scheme-independent leading IR divergences with- out introducing a regulator, by integrating certain residue forms along desingularizations of permanent pinch varieties. Our methods work for massless and mixed massive-massless inte- grals, for non-planar integrals with arbitrary numerators as well as for integrals with higher (integer) powers of propagators. We use this analysis to determine leading singular terms in some examples of box integrals. We further study examples of finite integrals at higher loops, and in particular provide a basis for finite non-evanescent integrals in a non-planar two-loop five-point topology.

</details>


### [109] [QFT on flat, compact, orientable manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02330)
*Sebestyen Nagy,Daniel Nogradi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文受近期关于有限温度旋转时空可由特定平坦紧致可定向流形（不同于T^4）描述的观测启发，系统编目了所有此类4维流形及其性质，共发现26种平坦紧致可定向4维流形，其中23种为自旋流形可定义费米子与Dirac算子，7种适用于有限体积周期性盒子在有限温度下旋转的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明有限温度下旋转时空可由特定的平坦紧致可定向流形而非通常的T^4来描述，因此需要对所有此类流形进行全面编目，以理解旋转周期盒子的拓扑与几何结构。

Method: 对所有平坦、紧致、可定向的4维流形进行分类编目，系统分析其自旋结构、费米子定义可行性以及适用于有限温度旋转周期盒子条件的流形子集。

Result: 总共鉴定出26种平坦紧致可定向的4维流形，其中23种为自旋流形，支持费米子和Dirac算子；其中7种适用于有限体积周期性盒子在有限温度下的旋转场景。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在旋转有限体积系统中进行量子场论计算提供了完整的流形分类框架，明确了哪些拓扑结构可用于描述有限温度旋转系统的物理。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation that rotating space-times at finite temperature can be described by particular flat, compact, orientable manifolds, which are different from $\mathbb T^4$, we catalogue all such 4-manifolds and their properties. There are in total 26 flat, compact, orientable 4-manifolds, 23 of them spin-manifolds, so fermions and a Dirac operator can also be defined on these. Seven of them are appropriate for finite volume periodic boxes undergoing rotation at finite temperature.

</details>


### [110] [Surprises of Klein paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02370)
*T. R. Ahmadov,E. T. Akhmedov,V. I. Lapushkin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究1+1维有质量狄拉克费米子在背景电场中的二次量子化理论，分析了类似平行电容器反向电场的势能配置下的Schwinger效应电流问题。作者证明在此类势能瞬时接通的情形下，当0<V<2m时电流算子的极限期望值为零，与直觉预期（两个电容器各自产生不等电流从而总电流非零）相反；仅当V>2m时才出现非零电流，并进一步探讨了无限深墙与深阱势下的高斯近似结果及其与超临界核衰变的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战并纠正关于Schwinger效应中多重平行电场区域叠加时总电流的朴素直觉判断，精确刻画在特定势能参数区间内电流的严格行为，揭示量子场论中粒子产生与湮灭之间更微妙的机制。

Method: 采用1+1维二次量子化的狄拉克费米子理论，在背景电场中考虑含参数V0>2m和V+V0（0<V<2m）的势能结构，利用瞬时接通势的方式，计算电流算子当t→+∞时的极限期望值进行严格分析，并借助高斯近似处理无限墙与深阱情形。

Result: 证明当0<V<2m时，电流算子的极限期望值实际为零，推翻了分别计算每个电容器电流再求总和的朴素预期；非零电流仅在V>2m时出现。在无限墙与深阱势（V0>2m）下，高斯近似中电流期望值同样为零。

Conclusion: Schwinger效应中电流的最终行为不能简单地按各电场区域独立产生粒子再相加减来处理，而是受整体势能结构及其参数区间的严格控制；这一结论对理解超临界核的衰变机制具有相关启示。

Abstract: We consider second quantized $1+1$ dimensional Dirac fermions with mass $m$ in background electric fields. This theory mimics the following situations. We consider two parallel capacitors with opposite directions of the electric field $E$ in them. The potential energy difference in one of them is equal to $V_0 > 2m$ and in the other to $V+V_0$, with $0<V<2m$. According to general physical understanding of the Schwinger effect, each capacitor separately will create pairs and there will be oppositely directed currents, which will not be equal to each other. The total current will not vanish. In this paper we show that this is too naive an observation: the limiting expectation value of the current operator, as $t\to +\infty$, is actually zero, if the above form of the potential with $0<V<2m$ is instantaneously turned on. The non-vanishing current is obtained only if $V>2m$. We also consider the same theory in the presence of the infinite wall and a deep well $V_0>2m$. We show that in such a potential the limiting expectation value of the current is zero in the Gaussian approximation. We discuss the relation of these observations to the Schwinger effet and to the decay of supercritical nuclei.

</details>


### [111] [Localization and Abelianization of Strings on Group Manifolds: The Simply Connected Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02425)
*Yongchao Lü*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致、连通且单连通的简单李群上，环面上Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）模型配分函数的局域化公式。作者在Murthy和Witten原始局域化处理中发现了缺失因子，并证明其与哈密顿形式所得结果一致。该缺失因子源自Wess-Zumino振幅的阿贝尔化，其约化为与极大环面相关的Narain格点CFT中的平坦Kalb-Ramond B场和乐。文章进一步分析了环面配分函数的点粒子极限，此时WZW模型退化为群流形上的量子力学，并重现了Frenkel的热核迹公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证和修正WZW模型在环面上的配分函数局域化公式——Murthy和Witten的原始局域化处理存在缺失因子，需要识别其来源并将其与哈密顿形式的结果统一，从而建立局域化结果与阿贝尔理论格点描述之间的直接联系。

Method: 通过分析Wess-Zumino振幅的阿贝尔化过程，将缺失因子追溯到极大环面相关的Narain格点CFT中平坦Kalb-Ramond B场和乐；然后研究环面配分函数的点粒子极限，将WZW模型约化为群流形上的量子力学，并与Frenkel的热核迹公式进行比较验证。

Result: 识别了原始局域化处理中缺失的因子，证明其与哈密顿形式结果一致；确定了缺失因子来源于Wess-Zumino振幅阿贝尔化后产生的平坦Kalab-Ramond B场和乐；在点粒子极限下成功重现了Frenkel的热核迹公式。

Conclusion: 局域化公式与哈密顿形式描述的WZW环面配分函数一致，缺失因子的格点来源揭示了局域化处理与对应阿贝尔理论的Narain格点CFT描述之间的深层联系；点粒子极限进一步验证了该公式的正确性。

Abstract: We study the localization formula for the partition function of the Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) model on the torus for compact, connected, and simply connected simple Lie groups. We identify a missing factor in the original localization treatment of Murthy and Witten and show that it agrees with the result obtained from the Hamiltonian formulation. We trace its origin to the abelianization of the Wess--Zumino amplitude, which reduces to a flat Kalb--Ramond B--field holonomy in a Narain lattice CFT associated with the maximal torus. This establishes a direct relation between the localization result and the lattice description of the corresponding abelian theory. We further analyze the point--particle limit of the torus partition function, in which the WZW model reduces to quantum mechanics on the group manifold, and reproduce Frenkel's heat--kernel trace formula.

</details>


### [112] [An Infinite Family of Non-Rational VOAs from Strongly Coupled 4d Higgsless SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02437)
*Hongliang Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一族具有平凡Higgs分支的强耦合四维N=2超共形场论(A2,D3m+1) Argyres-Douglas理论，提出其对应的顶点算子代数(VOA)为三场强生成的无限族非有理VOA——双代数A(4m+2)，并通过多种非平凡检验（共形反常、Schur指标精确相等和diagonal-gauging实现）验证了这一猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有平凡Higgs分支（"Higgsless"）的四维N=2 SCFT的数学结构，特别是通过VOA/顶点算子代数的语言来刻画这些强耦合理论。此前关于这类理论的VOA对应关系尚不清晰，作者旨在建立并验证这一对应关系，揭示这类特殊SCFT与简单VOA之间的联系。

Method: (1) 提出(A2,D3m+1)理论对应双代数A(4m+2) VOA的猜想；(2) 通过VOA计算共形反常a与c并验证与四维结果一致；(3) 构建diagonal-gauging实现，将理论分解为两个更简单的构建块，使Schur指标计算可处理；(4) 解析证明Schur指标与VOA超特征标精确相等；(5) 将结果推广到两参数族(A2s,D(2s+1)m+1)；(6) 特别分析(a,c)与a=c的子族(A2s,D2s+2)。

Result: 成功证明了(A2,D3m+1)的Schur指标与N=4 SU(2) SYM的指标仅在整体前因子和适当时标识别下一致；系统性地验证了VOA猜想并推广到更大的两参数族。揭示了(a,c)=(2,2)的Higgsless SCFT、diagonal gauging与强有限但非有理VOA之间的系统性联系。

Conclusion: 作者提出并验证了(A2,D3m+1) Argyres-Douglas理论与双代数A(4m+2) VOA之间的严格对应，并推广到更一般的(A2s,D(2s+1)m+1)族。该工作系统性地揭示了具有平凡Higgs分支的SCFT与强有限非有理VOA之间的密切联系，为理解这类特殊四维理论的代数结构提供了坚实证据。

Abstract: We study an infinite family of strongly coupled four-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ superconformal field theories (SCFTs) distinguished by a trivial Higgs branch, known as the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ Argyre-Douglas theories. We propose that their associated vertex operator algebras (VOAs) are the doublet algebras $\mathcal A(4m+2)$, an infinite family of non-rational vertex operator superalgebras with a remarkably simple strong generating set of only three fields. We provide several highly nontrivial checks of this proposal. In particular, we reproduce the four-dimensional conformal anomalies $a$ and $c$ from the VOA and analytically prove the exact equality between the Schur index of the SCFT and the supercharacter of the VOA. A key ingredient is a diagonal-gauging realization of the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ theories in terms of two simpler building blocks, which makes the Schur-index computation tractable. Remarkably, we find that the resulting Schur index of the $(A_2,D_{3m+1})$ theory coincides with that of $\mathcal N=4$ $SU(2)$ Super-Yang-Mills theory, up to an overall prefactor and an appropriate identification of fugacities. We also discuss a generalization to the two-parameter family $(A_{2s},D_{(2s+1)m+1})$, whose members likewise have trivial Higgs branches and admit diagonal-gauging realizations. A particularly interesting subfamily is $(A_{2s},D_{2s+2})$, for which the four-dimensional conformal anomalies coincide, $a=c$. Our results reveal a systematic connection between Higgsless SCFTs, diagonal gauging, and strongly finite but non-rational vertex operator algebras.

</details>


### [113] [Localization and Abelianization of Strings on Group Manifolds: The Non-simply Connected Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02472)
*Yongchao Lü*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非单连通紧致简单李群流形上的Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）模型配分函数。从Felder-Gawędzki-Kupiainen（FGK）的模不变配分函数出发，推导出一个局域化公式，该公式也可通过相应超对称WZW模型的超对称局域化直接获得。结果揭示了与非平凡目标群拓扑相关的多种拓扑效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的WZW模型研究多聚焦于单连通的紧致简单李群目标空间，但对于非单连通的紧致李群流形，其配分函数与拓扑效应尚未得到系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，统一刻画非单连通目标群情形下WZW模型中的拓扑贡献。

Method: 从FGK模不变配分函数出发推导局域化公式，并利用超对称WZW模型的超对称局域化方法交叉验证。分析中引入FGK余环刻画Wess-Zumino振幅，并通过Pfaffian线丛的完整群研究费米子Pfaffian的全局反常，借助相对Rochlin不变量进行描述。

Result: 推导出一个局域化公式，发现Wess-Zumino振幅由FGK余环控制，费米子Pfaffian表现出与Pfaffian线丛完整群相关的全局反常，由相对Rochlin不变量刻画。这些结果共同为具有非单连通目标群的WZW模型的半经典局域化中的拓扑贡献提供了统一描述。

Conclusion: 本文为带有非单连通紧致简单李群目标空间的WZW模型建立了系统的拓扑效应分析框架，统一了Wess-Zumino振幅与费米子全局反常的拓扑来源，推进了对这类模型中几何与拓扑相互作用的完整理解。

Abstract: We study the partition functions of Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) models with compact connected simple Lie group manifolds that are not simply connected. Starting from the modular-invariant partition function of Felder--Gawędzki--Kupiainen (FGK), we derive a localization formula, which can also be obtained directly by supersymmetric localization of the corresponding supersymmetric WZW model. Our results reveal a variety of topological effects associated with the nontrivial topology of the target group. In particular, the Wess--Zumino amplitude is governed by FGK cocycles, while the fermion Pfaffians exhibit global anomalies associated with the holonomies of Pfaffian line bundles, captured by relative Rochlin invariants. Together, these results provide a unified description of the topological contributions to the semiclassical localization of WZW models with non-simply connected target groups.

</details>


### [114] [Universal fingerprint of topological defect cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02521)
*Zi-Qiang Zhao,Nayun Jia,Zhang-Yu Nie,Hua-Bi Zeng,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个普适的拓扑缺陷标度律：在k>1/ξ区域，形状因子S_f正比于k^{-(d+p+2)}，该指数源自缺陷核心的V形尖点结构，与底层系统和动力学无关。通过数值模拟在四种典型框架中验证了这一标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然拓扑缺陷无处不在（从凝聚态物理到早期宇宙），且关于缺陷之间关联已存在许多普适标度定律（如Porod标度），但缺陷核心本身的"指纹"特征在很大程度上尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，寻找缺陷核心的普适标度行为。

Method: 作者从解析角度证明了缺陷核心的通用V形尖点结构导致形状因子的指数为-(d+p+2)，其中d为空间维度，p为缺陷余维数。随后通过数值模拟在四种典型框架中进行验证：(1)弱耦合区的含时Ginzburg-Landau方程；(2)弱耦合区的Gross-Pitaevskii方程；(3)强耦合区的规范/引力对偶模型；(4)宇宙学中Friedmann-Robertson-Walker背景下的Klein-Gordon方程。

Result: 数值模拟在四种截然不同的理论框架中均验证了标度律S_f ∝ k^{-(d+p+2)}在k>1/ξ区域的成立，证明该标度律具有极强的普适性，不受具体系统和动力学机制的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个研究拓扑缺陷的新探针，可应用于从超导体到宇宙学相变等广泛系统中，为理解和探测拓扑缺陷核心结构提供了全新的普适标度工具。

Abstract: Topological defects are ubiquitous in physics, arising from condensed matter physics to the early universe. Although there exist many universal scaling laws for correlations between topological defects, such as Porod scaling, the fingerprint of topological defect cores has remained largely unexplored. Here, we discover a universal scaling law in the region $k>1/ξ$, where $ξ$ is the healing length of the topological defects, taking the scaling of the form factor $S_f \propto k^{-(d+p+2)}$, where $d$ is the spatial dimension and $p$ is the defect codimension. We analytically prove that this exponent originates from a universal V-shaped cusp at the defect core and is independent of the underlying system and dynamics. Numerical simulations verify this scaling law in four typical frameworks: the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau and Gross-Pitaevskii equations in the weak-coupling regime, the gauge/gravity duality model in the strong-coupling regime, and the Klein-Gordon equation in the Friedmann-Robertson-Walker background in cosmology. Our work provides a new probe for studying topological defects in systems ranging from superconductors to cosmological phase transitions.

</details>


### [115] [Scale-separated AdS$_2$ flux vacua from type II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02814)
*George Tringas,Timm Wrase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了具有参数化尺度分离的AdS₂通量真空，通过在G₂结构轨道流形乘圆上紧致化II型弦论，并引入涂抹的时空填充O1/O5或O4/O8平面以及NSNS和RR通量，实现了KK尺度与AdS₂曲率尺度的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于两个问题：紧致化的内部尺度能否与外部曲率尺度参数化解耦，以及能否通过全息方法探测此类真空。构造这种尺度分离的AdS₂真空对于理解全息对应和紧致化中的尺度层次具有基础意义。

Method: 在G₂结构轨道流形乘以圆的背景上紧致化II型弦论，加入涂抹的时空填充O1/O5（IIB）或O4/O8（IIA）平面，配合NSNS和RR通量。推导二维dilaton-引力有效理论，分析十维Killing旋量方程，并讨论相关的T-对偶关系。

Result: 在IIB中通过无界的F₅和H₇通量、在IIA中通过无界的F₄、F₈和H₇通量实现参数化控制。大通量量子数下弦耦合参数性变弱，所有内部体半径在弦单位下参数性变大，KK尺度与AdS₂曲率尺度分离。识别了保持二维N=(1,1)超对称的参数化分支，并说明这些真空与扩展超对称尺度分离no-go定理的相容性。

Conclusion: 首次在AdS₂中实现了参数化尺度分离的通量真空，为研究尺度分离与全息对应提供了新的几何构造平台，并通过T-对偶关联到其他类型结构空间上的紧致化方案。

Abstract: Motivated by the question of whether the internal scales of a compactification can be parametrically decoupled from the external curvature scale, and by the possibility of probing such vacua holographically, we construct the first AdS$_2$ flux vacua with parametric scale separation. We compactify type II string theory on a $G_2$ structure orbifold times a circle in the presence of smeared spacetime-filling O1/O5-planes or O4/O8-planes, together with NSNS and RR fluxes. We derive the two-dimensional dilaton-gravity effective theory and exhibit families of vacua in which unbounded $F_5$ and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIB, while unbounded $F_4$, $F_8$, and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIA. For large flux quanta, the string coupling becomes parametrically weak, all internal bulk radii become parametrically large in string units, and the Kaluza--Klein scale separates from the $\mathrm{AdS}_2$ curvature scale. We analyze the ten-dimensional Killing-spinor equations and identify parametric branches preserving $\mathcal N=(1,1)$ supersymmetry in two dimensions. We explain how our geometrically scale-separated AdS$_2$ vacua are compatible with a recent no-go theorem for scale separation in theories with extended supersymmetry. Finally, we discuss various T-dualities leading to other type IIB and type IIA compactifications on $G_2$ structure spaces times a circle and $SU(3)$ structure spaces times a two-torus.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-damped modes of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström black holes from exact WKB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02816)
*Prisco Lo Chiatto,Sebastian Schenk,Nils Wagner,Felix Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了精确WKB方法在分析近极端Reissner–Nordström黑洞零阻尼模（ZDM）谱中的强大能力，通过对径向特征值问题构建完整的Stokes几何和精确量子化条件（EQC），实现了比以往研究更高阶精度的Voros符号解析计算，为将精确WKB方法推广到其他系统的ZDM谱研究提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞的环（ringdown）晚期动力学被认为由零阻尼模主导，其衰减率相对普通准正则模呈参数性抑制。精确描述ZDM谱对于理解近极端黑洞的引力波信号和晚期衰减行为至关重要，而现有方法的计算精度仍有提升空间。

Method: 采用精确WKB（exact WKB）方法系统分析近极端Reissner–Nordström黑洞上无质量、中性标量场的径向特征值问题。具体步骤包括：推导径向特征值问题的完整Stokes几何，建立对应的精确量子化条件（EQC），并解析计算EQC中涉及的Voros符号以获得ZDM谱。

Result: 作者实现了对ZDM谱的解析计算，其精度高于此前的研究，且该计算方法具有系统性可改进性（systematically improvable），证明了精确WKB方法在该问题上的优越性。

Conclusion: 这项工作作为概念验证，表明精确WKB方法为研究近极端黑洞ZDM谱提供了异常强大的框架，未来可推广至其他系统（如旋转黑洞或其他场类型）的ZDM谱研究。

Abstract: The late-time ringdown dynamics of near-extremal black holes (BHs) are expected to be dominated by zero-damped modes (ZDMs), whose decay rates are parametrically suppressed relative to those of ordinary quasinormal modes. In this paper, we demonstrate that exact WKB methods provide an exceptionally powerful framework for analyzing the ZDM spectrum of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström (RN) BHs. Focusing on massless, neutral scalar modes propagating on an RN background, we present the full Stokes geometry derived from the radial eigenvalue problem and establish the corresponding exact quantization condition (EQC). Our analytic computation of the Voros symbols entering the EQC achieves higher-order accuracy for the ZDM spectrum compared to previous studies and is systematically improvable. Ergo, this work serves as a proof of concept for investigations of ZDM spectra of other systems using exact WKB methods.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>
