### [132] [Extended Body Propagators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01829)
*Rory O'Dwyer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ansoldi等人提出的扩展体传播子对应于环境维度D中概率密度函数为f(A)=C·A·(α²+A²)^(-D/2)的随机变量的特征函数，其中尺度α=√(b/2)。该面积测度可作为从一维Polyakov路径积分到二维单纯复形扩展的自然候选，并证明其恰为三角形面积的分布律，还给出了将管状世界面测度约化为此三角形测度的论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ansoldi、Erbin和Stanford等人分别提出了三种看似截然不同的扩展体传播子，各自产生于完全不同的设定中，但都可视为Polyakov路径积分的一种实现。作者希望揭示这些传播子之间的深层联系，特别是识别Ansoldi传播子对应的随机变量及其概率分布，并为扩展体路径空间测度向二维单纯复形的推广建立起自然基础。

Method: 本文通过数学分析证明Ansoldi传播子正是具有特定概率密度函数f(A)=C·A·(α²+A²)^(-D/2)的随机变量的特征函数，指数由环境维度D单独确定。作者经过广泛文献调研确认该面积测度的新颖性，并进一步证明该面积测度恰为三角形面积的分布律（其中两条边来自伴随手稿中一维Polyakov积分得到的测度），最后给出一个部分严格的论证，将两个任意边界曲线间管状世界面的测度约化为此三角形的测度。

Result: Ansoldi传播子被识别为特定概率分布的特征函数，其指数仅由环境维度D决定；Erbin等人的壳上两点振幅以及JT引力与其D=4情形在精确的意义上一致；所构造的面积测度是新颖的，并自然成为未来二维单纯复形扩展的候选；该面积测度证明是三角形面积的精确分布律；若干边界曲线间管状世界面测度被约化为此三角形测度。

Conclusion: 本文在Ansoldi、Erbin和Stanford的传播子之间建立了新的联系，将其统一在Polyakov路径积分的框架下。所构造的面积测度不仅具有新颖性，更为扩展体路径空间测度向二维单纯复形和一般边界情形的推广提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Three seemingly distinct extended-body propagators devised by Ansoldi et al., Erbin et al., and Stanford et al.\ respectively arise in completely different settings, though each can be considered a realization of the Polyakov path integral. This paper proves that the Ansoldi propagator corresponds to the characteristic function of the random variable with the probability density function $f(A)=C\,A\,(α^{2}+A^{2})^{-D/2}\mathbf{1}_{A>0}$ in ambient dimension $D$, of scale $α=\sqrt{b/2}$. The exponent is fixed by the dimension and by nothing else in the Ansoldi/geometric comparison; the on-shell two-point amplitude of Erbin et al. and JT gravity agree with its $D=4$ member in the senses made precise below. After an extensive literature review, the author believes that this observation and constructed area measure is novel. The area measure will, however, emerge as the natural candidate for the extension of the earlier non-extended body path space measures to two dimensional simplicial complexes. We further show that the area measure of this paper is exactly the law of the area of a triangle, two of whose sides are drawn from the measure obtained in the companion manuscripts for the one-dimensional Polyakov integral. We close with an argument, rigorous in part, which reduces the measure of tubular worldsheets between two arbitrary boundary curves to that of this triangle.

</details>


### [133] [Applying the convolution theorem to the two-dimensional magnetic inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01862)
*Michael T. M. Woodley*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对二维磁学反演问题中应用卷积定理求解电流密度重建的关键数学细节进行了系统梳理和澄清，重点推导了Biot-Savart定律中卷积核的傅里叶变换闭式解，并据此通过卷积定理获得傅里叶域中电流密度与磁场数据及其他变量的函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁学反演问题（从磁场数据重建电流密度）的数学细节常被零散处理，缺乏系统化的整理与澄清。研究者需要明确卷积核傅里叶变换的闭式解，才能使卷积定理在该问题中的应用路径清晰、可复现。

Method: 作者系统收集并阐明二维磁学反演中应用卷积定理求解问题所需的关键数学细节，着重推导Biot-Savart定律所含卷积核的傅里叶变换闭式解，进而借助卷积定理，将反演转化为傅里叶域中由磁场数据傅里叶变换及其他变量获得电流密度傅里叶变换的代数运算。

Result: 给出了Biot-Savart定律卷积核的傅里叶变换闭式表达式，并基于卷积定理建立了傅里叶变换电流密度与傅里叶变换磁场数据及其他变量之间的明确函数关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维磁学反演问题的卷积定理求解法提供了完整的数学基础与形式化推导框架，使重构算法在傅里叶域中的实现更加清晰可行。

Abstract: A collection and clarification is given of the key mathematical details of the method of applying the convolution theorem as part of the solution to the two-dimensional magnetic inverse problem: reconstructing current density from magnetic field data. Particular emphasis is given to obtaining a closed-form solution for the Fourier transform of the convolution kernel that features in the Biot-Savart law. Having done this, the Fourier-transformed current density is obtained, via the convolution theorem, as a function of Fourier-transformed magnetic field data and other variables.

</details>


### [134] [Fractional oscillators with fractional damping in the presence of external forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01883)
*Fernando Olivar-Romero,Oscar Rosas-Ortiz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了显式分数阶阻尼项下的分数阶振子对外部力的响应。该三参数分数阶微分方程(Caputo意义下)存在精确解，其中两个分数参数与内禀耗散机制相关（产生持续能量耗散），第三个分数参数描述外部力驱动下的阻尼项。通过Laplace变换求解，分析了无外力、恒力、正弦力和阶跃力等典型情形。结果表明恒力驱动后系统达到固定位置，正弦驱动则呈现持续振荡行为，说明外部驱动力决定分数阻尼振子的长期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望理解当动力定律中包含显式分数阶阻尼项时，分数阶振子如何对外部力产生响应。这一问题的物理背景与分数阶动力学和粘弹性材料的牛顿-斯科特-布莱尔模型相关，具有基础理论意义和应用价值。

Method: 采用三参数分数阶微分方程(Caputo意义下)描述系统，其中两个分数参数表征内禀耗散机制，第三个参数表征与外部力对抗的阻尼项。利用Laplace变换对微分方程进行精确求解，外部力的Laplace变换定义了解的轮廓。分别考察了无外力、恒定力、正弦力和阶跃力等具体情形。

Result: 在恒定量驱动力下，系统经瞬态过程后达到固定位置；在正弦驱动力下，系统表现出持续的振荡行为。这表明外部驱动力的性质直接决定了分数阻尼振子的长期动态行为。

Conclusion: 外部驱动力决定分数阻尼振子的长期行为——不同形式的外力会导致截然不同的终态：恒力导致静止平衡，正弦力导致持续振荡，分数阻尼参数则影响瞬态演化特征。

Abstract: We investigate how a fractional oscillator reacts to external forces when the dynamic law includes a damping term that is explicitly fractional. The corresponding three-parametric fractional differential equation (in the Caputo sense) admits exact solution. Two of the fractional parameters are associated with the intrinsic dissipation mechanism that produces continuous dissipation of energy. The third fractional parameter characterizes the damping term against which the oscillator is driven by the external force. When external time-dependent forces are included, the fractional dynamic law is associated with the Newton-Scott-Blair model of viscoelastic materials. The Laplace transform of the external force defines the profile of the solution; specific examples include the absence of external forces as well as constant, sinusoidal and stepped external forces. With a constant driving force, the system reaches a fixed position after a transient period. With a sinusoidal driving force, the system exhibits persistent oscillatory behavior. These results suggest that the external driving force determines the long-term behavior of the fractionally damped oscillator.

</details>


### [135] [On the Convergence of Metadynamics with Gaussian Hills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01934)
*Andrey Badanin,Olga Rogacheva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低维（一维）集合变量下Metadynamics增强采样方法的收敛性局限。通过将演化问题归约为复制子型微分方程进行分析，发现周期性集合变量下Metadynamics可证明收敛；但当集合变量定义在有限区间上时，平稳解仅以弱意义存在为有限原子测度，导致Metadynamics缺乏清晰的准稳态而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Metadynamics是分子建模中广泛使用的增强采样方法，但现有收敛性结果对高斯势垒（Gaussian hills）的局限性缺乏深入理解，特别是在低维集合变量情形下的收敛行为尚不明确，需要严格的理论分析来阐明其适用范围与失效条件。

Method: 将一维集合变量下的Metadynamics演化问题归约为复制子型（replicator-type）微分方程，并对其长期行为进行数学分析。分别考察周期性集合变量和有限区间集合变量两种情形下解的收敛性质，利用弱收敛和原子测度的数学工具进行分析。

Result: 对于周期性集合变量，证明了Metadynamics的收敛性。然而当集合变量定义在有限区间时，平稳解仅以弱意义存在为有限原子测度，微分方程的解弱收敛到该测度。由此Metadynamics由于缺乏清晰的准稳态而失效；只有当自由能在有限区间外于足够大区域（相比σ）近似恒定时，才可能在"INTERVAL"框架中捕捉到准稳态瞬态解。

Conclusion: Metadynamics在有限区间集合变量下的有效性受限于边界效应：标准实现无法建立清晰的准稳态，仅当区间外存在足够大且自由能近似平坦的区域时才能实现有效采样。这表明实践中需谨慎选择集合变量的定义域及其边界条件。

Abstract: Metadynamics is a class of enhanced-sampling methods that is widely used in molecular modeling. Here, we consider metadynamics with a one-dimensional collective variable and explore the limitations of using Gaussian hills in light of existing convergence results. We reduce the corresponding evolution problem to a replicator-type differential equation and analyze its long-time behavior. For a periodic collective variable, we prove the convergence of metadynamics. However, the situation is fundamentally different when the collective variable is defined on a finite interval. In this case, the stationary solution only exists in the weak sense as a finite atomic measure. The solution to the differential equation weakly converges to it. Consequently, metadynamics is ineffective due to the absence of a clear quasi-stationary state. A quasi-stationary, transient solution can only be captured in the "INTERVAL" framework if the free energy outside the finite interval remains nearly constant across sufficiently large regions compared to $σ$.

</details>


### [136] [Superintegrability of stratified symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01953)
*Zhuo Chen,Kai Jiang,Nicolai Reshetikhin,Husileng Xiao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了分层辛空间上哈密顿系统的超可积性概念，并证明SU(3)的自旋Calogero-Moser-Sutherland（sCMS）系统是超可积的，其中相空间是通过紧致李群余切丛的哈密顿约化得到的分层辛空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展超可积性的理论框架，使其适用于分层辛空间上的哈密顿系统，而非仅限于光滑辛流形上的传统可积系统；同时希望对自旋Calogero-Moser-Sutherland系统建立超可积性结论。

Method: 首先定义分层辛空间上的超可积性概念。然后以紧致李群（具体为SU(3)）余切丛的哈密顿约化来构造分层辛空间作为sCMS系统的相空间，并通过该框架证明sCMS系统在该空间上满足超可积性条件。

Result: SU(3)的自旋Calogero-Moser-Sutherland系统被证明是超可积的。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一般性分层辛空间超可积性的定义，并将其成功应用于证明SU(3)的sCMS系统具有超可积性，为更广泛李群上的sCMS系统可积性研究提供了理论框架和方法基础。

Abstract: We define the notion of superintegrability of a Hamiltonian system on a stratified symplectic space. We focus on spin Calogero-Moser-Sutherland (sCMS) systems, where the phase space is a stratified symplectic space obtained by the Hamiltonian reduction of the cotangent bundle over a compact Lie group, and demonstrate that the sCMS systems for $SU(3)$ are superintegrable.

</details>


### [137] [Heat transfer problem of a dense gas described by the Enskog equation with a modification of the Enskog factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02276)
*Shigeru Takata,Soma Sakata,Masanari Hattori*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种Enskog方程（原始Enskog方程与最近提出的具有修正Enskog因子的版本）在平行板间稠密气体传热问题中的定量差异。结果表明，该修正对密度、温度和热流矢量的影响有限，但在某些严苛参数设置下对应力张量的影响明显可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Enskog方程缺乏H定理，而最近在Phys. Rev. E上提出的修正版Enskog方程（含修正Enskog因子）已建立了H定理。然而，该修正对实际物理量（如宏观量）的影响程度尚不清楚，需要定量评估以判断该修正在实际应用中的合理性。

Method: 以平行板间稠密气体的热传递问题为基准算例，分别采用原始Enskog方程和修正版（含修正Enskog因子）Enskog方程进行求解与数值比较，通过对比密度、温度、热流矢量和应力张量等宏观量来量化两方程之间的差异。

Result: 修正Enskog因子对密度场、温度场和热流矢量的影响相当有限；但在某些较严苛的参数设置下，该修正对应力张量产生了可见的影响。

Conclusion: 修正版Enskog方程在保证H定理成立的前提下，对大部分宏观量的影响可忽略不计，仅在极端参数条件下对应力张量产生显著偏差。这为该修正版的采用提供了支持，同时也提示在需要精确应力预测的严苛工况下需谨慎处理其与原始Enskog方程之间的差异。

Abstract: Heat transfer in a dense gas between two parallel plates is studied for assessing the quantitative difference of two variants of the Enskog equation, i.e., the original Enskog equation and the Enskog equation with a modified Enskog factor recently proposed in Phys. Rev. E \textbf{111}, 065108 (2025). The main advantage of the latter is that the H theorem, which is lacking in the former, has been established. The influence of the modification is assessed by comparing the macroscopic quantities in details. It is found that the influence of this modification is quite limited in the density, temperature, and heat flow vector. However, the influence on the stress tensor is visible in some severe setting of parameters.

</details>


### [138] [Precise spectral asymptotics, exponential localization, and spectral gap estimates for the three-boson lattice Schrödinger operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02488)
*Abdikhurayra Toshturdiev,Abdumalik Eshniyozov,Janikul Abdullaev,Mikhail Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维整数格上具有成对接触相互作用的三玻色子薛定谔算子。在强耦合极限下，精确刻画了零总准动量处两个束缚态的能量渐进行为、基态波函数的指数局域化性质（衰减率以ln(3μ)+O(1/μ)为上界），以及π准动量处奇偶子空间的对称性破缺现象——奇子空间仅产生虚拟能级而偶子空间支撑唯一束缚态。从零动量两个束缚态到π动量一个束缚态的约化保持了谱流总量不变，是晶格特有的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解晶格上多体玻色子系统的谱理论和束缚态结构，特别是在强相互作用极限下的精确行为。该研究不仅具有数学物理层面的理论意义，而且对光学晶格中少体玻色子系统的量子模拟有直接指导意义。

Method: 采用Birman-Schwinger算子的不变子空间分解方法；使用离散Agmon比较方法和Paley-Wiener定理建立基态波函数的指数局域化；利用Krein-Rutman定理保证基态的唯一性和严格正性。

Result: (1) 零准动量下：基态能量为-3μ+6+O(1/μ)，第一激发态能量为-μ+C+O(1/μ)，其中C=4-δ≈3.96458，δ由包含晶格格林函数的超越方程b₀(δ)=1/(1+δ)确定，谱隙为2μ+O(1)。(2) 基态波函数呈指数局域化，衰减率上界为ln(3μ)+O(1/μ)。(3) π准动量下：奇子空间仅产生虚拟能级（特征值阶为1/μ但达不到Birman-Schwinger阈值），偶子空间支撑唯一束缚态，能量为-2μ+6+8/μ+O(1/μ²)，谱隙为μ-2-8/μ+O(1/μ²)。

Conclusion: 强耦合极限下晶格三玻色子系统的束缚态数目随准动量变化（零动量两个、π动量一个），但总谱流保持不变，这是连续空间中所不具备的晶格特有现象。研究结果为光学晶格少体量子模拟提供了精确的谱理论预测。

Abstract: We study the three-boson Schrodinger operator on the two-dimensional integer lattice with pairwise contact interactions. First, we obtain precise asymptotics of the two bound states below the essential spectrum at zero total quasimomentum in the strong-coupling limit mu to infinity: the ground state energy is -3 mu + 6 + O(1/mu), and the first excited state energy is - mu + C + O(1/mu), where C = 4 - delta approximately 3.96458, with delta determined by the transcendental equation b0(delta) = 1/(1+delta) involving the lattice Green's function. The associated spectral gap is 2 mu + O(1). Second, using the discrete Agmon comparison method and the Paley-Wiener theorem, we establish exponential localization of the ground-state wavefunction with a logarithmic upper bound on the decay rate alpha(mu) at most ln(3 mu) + O(1/mu), reflecting the bounded nature of the lattice dispersion; the sharp asymptotic rate is conjectured. Third, at quasimomentum pi, the parity symmetry is preserved, but the odd quadratic form becomes positive definite with a unique eigenvalue of order 1/mu that never reaches the Birman-Schwinger threshold, so the odd subspace yields only a virtual level. The even subspace supports exactly one bound state with energy -2 mu + 6 + 8/mu + O(1/mu^2), and the spectral gap to the essential spectrum is mu - 2 - 8/mu + O(1/mu^2). The reduction from two bound states at zero to one at pi preserves the total spectral flow and is a lattice-specific phenomenon. Our approach uses an invariant subspace decomposition of the Birman-Schwinger operator and the Krein-Rutman theorem to guarantee uniqueness and strict positivity of the ground state. These results have direct implications for quantum simulation of few-boson systems in optical lattices.

</details>


### [139] [Bimaterial Eshelby's inclusion problem for polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02539)
*Chunlin Wu,Huiming Yin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了双材料域中任意取向多面体夹杂在均匀本征应变下的闭合形式Eshelby张量。通过将两个Boussinesq位移势的体积积分约化为表面积分和初等线积分，弥补了双材料Green函数中缺失的域积分项，提供了完整的弹性和热弹性双材料Eshelby张量公式，并对其进行了多维度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双材料Eshelby张量解主要局限于特殊夹杂形状（如球体、立方体）或仅适用于膨胀型本征应变，原因是双材料Green函数在Kelvin解的调和与双调和势之外还包含两个Boussinesq位移势，使得一般情形的域积分难以解析求解。这限制了双材料域中任意取向、任意形状夹杂问题的准确分析。

Method: 将包含两个Boussinesq位移势的体积积分通过数学变换约化为表面积分，进而进一步约化为初等线积分，从而以闭合形式获得这些缺失的域积分项，结合已知的Kelvin解部分构造出完整的双材料Eshelby张量。

Result: 给出了完整的弹性和热弹性双材料Eshelby张量闭合公式。通过与平行于双材料界面的球形和立方体夹杂的解析解对比，以及与倾斜立方体的有限元结果对比，验证了公式的正确性。奇异性分析表明：当夹杂与双材料界面分离时，界面相关贡献保持正则；当夹杂的边或顶点接触界面时，会产生额外的对数奇异性，但不会提高主导奇异性阶数。

Conclusion: 该研究为双材料域中任意取向多面体夹杂提供了完整且通用的Eshelby张量闭合解，突破了现有方法在夹杂形状和本征应变类型上的限制，为不均匀材料的弹性和热弹性夹杂分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents the closed-form Eshelby's tensor for an arbitrarily oriented polyhedral inclusion in a bimaterial domain under general uniform eigenstrain. Existing bimaterial solutions are mainly restricted to special inclusion shapes or dilatational eigenstrains, because the bimaterial Green's function contains two Boussinesq's displacement potentials in addition to the harmonic and biharmonic potentials in Kelvin's solution. This paper derives the missing domain integrals of the two Boussinesq's potentials by reducing the volume integrals to surface and elementary line integrals. The formulae provide the complete elastic and thermoelastic bimaterial Eshelby's tensors, which are verified against analytical solutions for spherical and cuboidal inclusions parallel to the bimaterial interface, and finite element results of an inclined cuboid. Singularity analysis demonstrates that the interface-related contribution remains regular when the inclusion is separated from the bimaterial interface, while additional logarithmic singularities arise when an edge or vertex touches the interface without increasing the dominant singularity order.

</details>


### [140] [Ruelle--Pollicott Theory for Metastable Systems: A Unified Framework for Tipping Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02614)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Valerio Lucarini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Ruelle–Pollicott（RP）谱理论，为随机、多稳态或亚稳态系统发展了一种临界转变（tipping point）预警的通用理论，指出早期预警信号是RP谱块、可观测量和扰动方向三者的联合属性，而非简单的谱隙闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的临界减速（critical slowing down）范式仅在简单平衡分岔附近有效，并非随机、多稳态或亚稳态系统的通用理论。需要一个更一般的框架来理解什么条件下会出现可靠的早期预警信号，以及经典信号何时可能产生误导。

Method: 从亚椭圆Itô扩散的Kolmogorov生成元的Ruelle–Pollicott谱出发，分解不变统计量对各谱块的敏感性。每个贡献因式化为谱分母与残差（residue）的乘积，其中残差耦合该谱块与可观测量和扰动方向。对亚稳态系统使用单个被杀灭问题（killed problem）导出Doob Q-过程（隔离阱内恢复）与逃逸时钟及始点加权目的地概率（控制阱间转移）两个互补的谱对象。

Result: 证明了RP谱隙闭合并非早期预警的充分条件，而残差增长可在无谱隙闭合时产生经典信号。在随机非正规系统中，RP谱精确冻结但经典预警指标仍比真实谱隙闭合时更警示，该效应被RP分解归因于残差增长。定义了指标N(f)，对可逆动力学N(f)≤1，因此N(f)>1可确证残差驱动的放大。在一维折叠中，恢复时间与阱间转移分别按(εc-ε)^(1/2)和(εc-ε)^(3/2)标度，从而区分分岔诱导与噪声诱导的临界转变。

Conclusion: RP谱方法提供了超越分岔理论的临界转变预警的严谨数学框架，将预警信号、扰动方向与可观测量的依赖关系系统化，并为区分不同机制（谱隙闭合vs残差增长、分岔诱导vs噪声诱导）提供了可量化判据。

Abstract: Tipping points---abrupt, potentially irreversible reorganizations of a system's statistical state---are commonly anticipated through critical slowing down: recovery slows, autocorrelation and variance rise, and spectra redden. This paradigm is powerful near simple equilibrium bifurcations but is not a general theory for stochastic, multistable, or metastable systems. We develop such a theory from the Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) spectrum of Kolmogorov generators of hypoelliptic Itô diffusions. Resolving the sensitivity of invariant statistics on RP spectral blocks shows that each contribution factorizes into a spectral denominator and a residue coupling the block to both the observable and perturbation direction. Small denominators permit large responses; residues produce them. Thus a closing RP gap is not sufficient for an early warning, while growing residues can generate the classical signature with no gap closure. An early-warning signal is therefore a property of a triple: RP block, observable, and perturbation direction. We demonstrate this on a stochastic non-normal system whose RP spectrum is exactly frozen, yet classical indicators become more alarming than during genuine gap closure. The RP decomposition attributes this to residue growth and yields an index $\mathcal{N}(f)$ satisfying $\mathcal{N}(f)\leq 1$ for reversible dynamics; hence $\mathcal{N}(f)>1$ certifies residue-driven amplification. For metastable systems, one killed problem yields two complementary spectral objects: the Doob $Q$-process isolates in-well recovery, while escape clocks and committor-weighted destination probabilities govern interwell transitions. In a one-dimensional fold, these scale as $(ε_c-ε)^{1/2}$ and $(ε_c-ε)^{3/2}$, separating bifurcation-induced from noise-induced tipping. Doob drift also connects to optimal Girsanov sampling.

</details>


### [141] [Asymptotic long-range order for the XY-model on random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02618)
*Margherita Disertori,Max Mihailescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机几何图上的经典XY模型。在n趋于无穷且ε以足够慢的速度趋于0时，当逆温度β以依赖于n和ε的速率趋于无穷，证明该模型在随机几何图上表现出长程序（两点函数下界远离零），且结果是退火（quenched）意义上的——以大概率成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机几何图作为捕捉底层区域几何性质的随机图模型，是理解无序系统中经典自旋模型（如XY模型）在几何约束下相变行为的自然框架。作者旨在证明随机几何图上的XY模型在低温极限下具有长程序，为在随机离散环境中建立磁性有序提供了理论保证。

Method: 核心证明路线包括两步：(1) 证明随机环境（随机几何图）以高概率足够正则（如具有良好的连通性和几何性质）；(2) 在此正则的环境上，利用凸性论证和Brascamp-Lieb不等式来建立两点函数的下界，从而证明长程序。β趋于无穷的速率需与n和ε适当匹配。

Result: 在β→∞（以依赖于n和ε的速率）的极限下，XY模型在随机几何图G_{n,ε}上的两点函数存在远离零的下界，即模型表现出长程序。该结果在随机环境下为quenched（淬火）意义下的——以大概率（概率趋于1）成立。

Conclusion: 作者成功证明了随机几何图上二维及以上XY模型在低温极限下的长程序行为，并通过凸性方法和Brascamp-Lieb不等式给出了严格的数学证明。该结果在随机环境中以高概率成立，为随机几何图上的连续对称自旋模型提供了首个长程序方面的严格结论。

Abstract: We study the classical $XY$-model on random geometric graphs $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$, which are obtained by sampling $n \in \mathbb{N}$ independent points in a finite domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, $d \geq 2$, and connecting two points by and edge if their distance is of order $\varepsilon > 0$. We refer to $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ as the random environment. Letting $\varepsilon \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$ at a sufficiently slow rate, these graphs capture the geometry of $Ω$.
  Denoting the inverse temperature by $β$, we show that in the limit $β\to \infty$ at a rate depending on $n$ and $\varepsilon$, the $XY$-model on $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ exhibits long range order in the sense that we prove a lower bound away from zero on the two-point function. Our result is quenched in the random environment: long-range order holds with large probability, converging to one as $n \to \infty$. To prove the statement, we show that with high probability the environment is sufficiently regular to apply a convexity argument and the Brascamp--Lieb inequality.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Curvature-driven spin transport in rank-two string-Carroll hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01634)
*Nikko John Leo Lobos,Reggie Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了秩二弦-卡罗尔极限下的规范应力-自旋Ward系统，用于固定无挠背景上的探测物质。系统保留了耦合的Riemann-自旋力、第二纵向动量投影以及纵向boost双矢量。作者在独立自旋幅度ς中提取线性项，并展开近视界参数λ=ε²，避免了被省略的O(ς²)热力学自旋反馈污染。对于混合应力和S^{λμν}=O(ςε^p)，p=0是唯一使诱导应力与曲率源在第一个规范Ward阶平衡的扇区。在光滑非极端静态球对称外部视界的规则分支上，局部归一化的纵向和横向自旋幅度遵循相同的稀释定律。对于一般barotrope，稳态系统简化为一个热力学求积和一个局部逆。对于仿射常数声速族p=αE+Π（0≤α<1），作者获得了显式闭式参数解、两个自旋通道的前导轮廓，以及远离声点的定点、线性自旋对焓、压力和快度的修正。作者证明了静态闭球Killing通量在规则伪规范改进下不变。在Reissner-Nordström中，响应在|Q|/M=2√2/3处消失；在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton族中，其零点为R_-/R_+=(1+a²)/2（0≤a<1）。Frenkel极限移除了该响应和纵向boost扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机是超越秩一卡罗尔自旋流体动力学，建立秩二弦-卡罗尔极限下的规范应力-自旋Ward系统，以在近视界探测物质物理中保留更完整的动力学结构（如耦合Riemann-自旋力、第二纵向动量投影和纵向boost bivector），并系统研究稳态自旋流体在球对称黑洞视界附近的响应与pseudo-gauge不变性。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 对规范应力-自旋Ward系统取秩二弦-卡罗尔极限；(2) 在独立自旋幅度ς中线性化，并利用近视界参数λ=ε²展开以避免非线性自旋反馈污染；(3) 分析不同p扇区以获得唯一平衡条件p=0；(4) 对一般barotrope进行降维简化为一热力学求积和一局部逆；(5) 对仿射族p=αE+Π求显式闭式参数解；(6) 研究规则pseudo-gauge改进下静态闭球Killing通量的不变性；(7) 在Reissner-Nordström及Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton背景下定位径向潮汐反转点。

Result: 主要结果表明：p=0是唯一使诱导应力与曲率源在首阶Ward序平衡的扇区；纵向与横向自旋幅度在规则分支上服从相同稀释定律；曲率力在O(ςλ²)阶首次出现；仿射族获得显式参数解及自旋通道轮廓；响应由R^{(L)}_h= (f₂+3f₁ψ₁)/2和纵向自旋通量控制；静态闭球Killing通量在规则pseudo-gauge改进下不变；RN背景下响应在|Q|/M=2√2/3消失，EMD族中零点为R_-/R_+=(1+a²)/2；Frenkel极限消除响应与纵向boost扇区。

Conclusion: 结论是该秩二弦-卡罗尔框架为近视界稳态探测物质的spin动力学提供了统一的规范不变描述，其响应行为具体取决于背景电荷参数并在特定比值下精确消零。Frenkel极限的退化行为揭示了纵向boost扇区与响应机制之间的本质联系。

Abstract: We derive the rank-two string-Carroll limit of the canonical stress--spin Ward system for probe matter on a fixed torsion-free background. Beyond rank-one Carroll spin hydrodynamics, this retains the coupled Riemann--spin force, a second longitudinal momentum projection, and the longitudinal boost bivector allowed only by a two-dimensional longitudinal kernel. We first extract the term linear in an independent spin amplitude $ς$ and then expand in the near-horizon parameter $λ=ε^2$, avoiding contamination by the omitted $\mathcal{O}(ς^2)$ thermodynamic spin feedback. For finite mixed stress and $S^{λμν}=\mathcal{O}(ςε^p)$, $p=0$ is the unique sector in which the induced stress and curvature source balance at the first generic Ward order. On the regular branch of a smooth nonextremal static spherical outer horizon, locally normalized longitudinal and transverse spin amplitudes obey the same dilution law, while the curvature force first appears at $\mathcal{O}(ςλ^2)$. For a general barotrope, the stationary system reduces to one thermodynamic quadrature and one local inverse. For the affine constant-sound-speed family $p=α\mathcal E+Π$, $0\leqα<1$, we obtain an explicit closed-form parametric solution, leading profiles for both spin channels, and fixed-radius, linear-spin corrections to enthalpy, pressure, and rapidity away from the sonic point. The response is governed by $\mathcal R^{(L)}_h=(f_2+3f_1ψ_1)/2$ and the longitudinal spin flux. We prove that the stationary closed-sphere Killing flux is invariant under regular pseudo-gauge improvements. In Reissner--Nordström, the response vanishes at the radial-tidal inversion $|Q|/M=2\sqrt{2}/3$; in the Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton family, its zero is $R_-/R_+=(1+a^2)/2$ for $0\leq a<1$. The Frenkel limit removes this response and the longitudinal boost sector.

</details>


### [143] [A Unified Treatment of the Self-Force Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01644)
*Beka Modrekiladze,Ira Z. Rothstein,Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）引入了形式体系，可系统地计算浸没在具有非零应力能量环境中的致密物体的引力自力。通过闭环时间路径积分，作者给出了一个普适的有效作用量，用于计算致密物体与环境相互作用产生的运动方程，并可推广到任意阶展开参数。结果表明，领头阶运动方程可普适地用环境的延迟应力-能量张量两点函数来表示，该作用量既可用于耗散力（动力学摩擦）也可用于守恒力的完全相对论性计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力自力计算在致密天体（如黑洞、中子星）浸没于各种环境（暗物质晕、星际介质、流体等）时的动力学研究中对天体物理具有重要意义。现有方法缺乏一个系统化、普适的处理框架，特别是在处理非真空环境中耗散与守恒力的同时还需保证相对论协变性。作者引入世界线有效场论结合闭环时间路径积分，旨在提供一个统一且可系统扩展的形式体系。

Method: 论文采用世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）结合闭环时间路径积分（closed time path integral）构建普适有效作用量。该方法可对任意阶相关展开参数计算致密物体的运动方程。领头阶结果用环境的延迟应力-能量张量两点函数表达，同时涵盖耗散与守恒力。作者将该框架分别应用于尘埃、无粘流体和相干场三种环境验证其有效性，并通过重整化群分析解释Chandrasekhar的"Coulomb Log"。

Result: 论文推导了普适的领头阶运动方程，结果与文献中已有的尘埃、无粘流体和相干场耗散力的计算结果一致。作者进一步证明了Chandrasekhar著名的"Coulomb Log"应被诠释为源于紫外发散的重整化群对数，该发散重整化了in-in作用量的耗散部分。在牛顿极限下，该对数对一般类型的环境和轨迹具有普适性，且它是耗散作用量重整化群流中的领头对数。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在存在非零应力能量环境的情况下计算引力自力提供了统一且系统的有效场论框架。最重要的概念性成果是将Coulomb Log重新诠释为紫外发散导致的重整化群对数，揭示其在牛顿极限下的普适性，表明耗散作用量存在此前未被探索的重整化群流结构。

Abstract: This paper introduces formalism, based on worldline effective field theory, which allows one to systematically calculate the gravitational self-force on a compact object immersed in an environment with non-vanishing stress energy. Using the closed time path integral we present a universal effective action that can be used to calculate the equations of motion of a compact object due to its interaction with the environment to any order in the relevant expansion parameters, the relative importance of which depends upon the choice of environment. We show that the leading order equations of motion can be universally written in terms of the retarded two-point function of the stress-energy tensor for the environment. The resulting action can be used to calculate both dissipative (dynamical friction) and conservative forces in a completely relativistic fashion. We demonstrate the utility of the result by calculating the dissipative force due to dust, an inviscid fluid, and a coherent field. Our results agree with those previously derived in the literature. We furthermore show that the famous ``Coulomb Log" found by Chandrasekhar should be interpreted as a renormalization group log due to a UV divergence that renormalizes the dissipative part of the in-in action. We then prove that this log is universal in the Newtonian limit in that its value is universal, for a generic class of environments and trajectories. This log is the leading log in an RG flow in the dissipative action that has yet to be explored.

</details>


### [144] [Parameter Space, Realistic Matter, and Universal Relations in Bose--Einstein Condensate Dark Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01646)
*M. Ilyas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了慢旋转玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）暗物质的致密星，通过联立求解TOV、Hartle偶极和Postnikov-Hinderer方程，对含Lee-Huang-Yang修正的多方状态方程进行系统扫描。发现均值场到修正的过渡平滑；在(boson质量、散射长度)参数空间中，2倍太阳质量上限与GW170817潮汐形变约束无法同时满足；I-Love普适关系在BEC模型中成立至0.13%，跨核物质、混合星和BEC暗星模型时约5%精度内收敛，而自束缚夸克星偏离高达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统揭示BEC暗物质致密星在旋转和潮汐形变下的结构特性，检验修正BEC状态方程对最大质量、潮汐形变和I-Love关系的影响，并评估该状态方程类别与现有天文观测约束（如2倍太阳质量脉冲星和GW170817潮汐形变）的相容性。

Method: 将慢旋转近似下的TOV方程、Hartle偶极方程和Postnikov-Hinderer潮汐形变方程联立数值求解，采用含Lee-Huang-Yang量子涨落修正的多方状态方程。连续扫描修正强度ζ(0~1.5)以及微观玻色子参数(质量m、散射长度a_s)，同时将求解器分别应用于核物质（SLy、APR4）、MIT袋夸克物质和两流体重子-暗物质混合星以进行交叉验证。

Result: (1) 修正强度扫描平滑，无隐藏相结构；(2) 该方程类无法同时满足2M⊙最大质量界和GW170817的潮汐形变界；(3) I-Love关系在BEC模型中精度达0.13%；(4) 求解器在核物质和夸克物质上复现已知结果（误差<1%）；(5) 混合星最大质量与中心暗物质分数呈非单调关系；(6) 核-混合-BEC模型的I-Love序列在约5%内收敛，而夸克星偏离达90%。

Conclusion: 修正型BEC状态方程无法与现有致密星观测约束完全相容；I-Love普适关系的普适性被进一步确认，但自束缚夸克物质构成的星体是显著例外。这暗示核/暗物质致密星与夸克星在内部结构上的本质差异会在观测量层面体现，为区分不同物态提供判别依据。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating Bose--Einstein condensate (BEC) dark stars by solving the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff, Hartle dipole, and Postnikov--Hinderer equations together for a polytropic equation of state with a Lee--Huang--Yang correction of strength $ζ$~\citep{Panotopoulos2026}. A continuous scan of $ζ$ from 0 to 1.5 shows the mean-field-to-corrected transition is smooth, with no hidden structure at intermediate values. Scanning the underlying boson parameters $(m,a_s)$ more broadly, we find that a $2\,M_\odot$ maximum-mass bound and a GW170817-like tidal bound $Λ_{1.4}\lesssim800$ cannot be satisfied simultaneously anywhere in this equation-of-state class. The $I$-Love universal relation holds to $0.13\%$ across twelve $(m,a_s,ζ)$ models, and the $ζ=0$ and $ζ=1$ sequences sit on opposite sides of the master curve by a consistent, non-random offset. Applied without modification to realistic nuclear matter (SLy, APR4), the same solver reproduces published maximum masses to within $1\%$; applied to self-bound MIT-bag quark matter it gives the expected mass--radius shape; and once extended to a two-fluid baryon-plus-dark-matter formalism, it shows that the maximum mass of a hybrid star is not a monotonic function of the central dark-matter fraction. Pooling $I$-Love sequences across nuclear, hybrid, and BEC dark-star models, we find they collapse onto a single curve to within about $5\%$, while self-bound quark stars sit far off it, departing by up to $90\%$.

</details>


### [145] [Late-time dark energy dynamics in $f(Q)$ gravity: A data-driven analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01650)
*Kshetrimayum Govind Singh,Kangujam Priyokumar Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称teleparallel f(Q)引力框架下，采用幂律形式f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}研究晚期暗能量动力学，并利用CC、DESI DR2 BAO和三种独立Ia型超新星汇编数据（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）对模型进行约束。结果表明该模型在统计上优于ΛCDM（根据AIC），推断的H0值彼此一致（约67-68 km/s/Mpc），重建的有效EoS呈现quintessence-like行为，减速参数为负，减速到加速的转变发生在0.680≤z_t≤0.745区间，Statefinder和Om(z)诊断均支持该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着晚期宇宙学观测精度不断提高，需要测试修正引力理论对宇宙加速膨胀的描述能力。DESI DR2 BAO数据与宇宙学计时器和Ia型超新星数据的组合提供了数据驱动的检验平台，促使作者研究对称teleparallel f(Q)引力中晚期暗能量动力学。

Method: 采用幂律形式f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}的对称teleparallel f(Q)引力模型，通过有效状态方程参数刻画宇宙演化，使用CC、DESI DR2 BAO以及三种独立Ia型超新星汇编（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）进行联合约束，并使用AIC/BIC准则与ΛCDM比较，同时运用Statefinder、Om(z)和更高阶宇宙学参数诊断进行交叉验证。

Result: 三种数据组合分别给出H0=67.60^{+1.60}_{-1.66}、67.08^{+1.66}_{-1.61}和68.16^{+1.46}_{-1.43} km/s/Mpc，彼此一致。AIC准则下f(Q)模型对所有数据组合均受青睐，BIC在两种组合下倾向ΛCDM而在CC+DESI DR2+Union3组合下无显著偏好。EoS呈quintessence行为，跃迁红移在0.680≤z_t≤0.745，Statefinder轨迹趋近ΛCDM固定点。

Conclusion: 幂律f(Q)模型能提供与ΛCDM竞争且可行的晚期宇宙加速膨胀描述，所有诊断指标一致支持quintessence-like行为，表明该模型是ΛCDM的有力替代方案。

Abstract: The increasing precision and complementarity of late-time cosmological observations provide new opportunities to test modified gravity against the observed expansion history of the universe. The combination of DESI DR2 BAO measurements with cosmic chronometers (CC) and independent Type Ia supernova compilations provides a data-driven test of cosmic acceleration. Motivated by this progress, we investigate late-time dark energy dynamics in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity by adopting the power-law form $f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}$ and characterizing cosmic evolution through the effective equation of state (EoS) parameter. The model is constrained using CC, DESI DR2 BAO and three independent Type Ia supernova compilations, namely Pantheon+, DES-SN5YR and Union3. The inferred Hubble constant values are mutually consistent, yielding $H_0=67.60^{+1.60}_{-1.66}$, $67.08^{+1.66}_{-1.61}$ and $68.16^{+1.46}_{-1.43}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, respectively. Statistically, the proposed $f(Q)$ model yields lower $χ^2_{\min}$ values and is favored by the AIC for all dataset combinations, whereas the BIC favors $Λ$CDM for the CC + DESI DR2 + Pantheon+ and CC + DESI DR2 + DES-SN5YR combinations and indicates no meaningful preference between the two models for the CC + DESI DR2 + Union3 dataset combinations. The reconstructed EoS indicates quintessence-like behavior, while the negative deceleration parameter confirms the current accelerated expansion. The transition from deceleration to acceleration occurs within $0.680\leq z_t\leq0.745$. Higher-order cosmographic parameters and the Statefinder and $Om(z)$ diagnostics consistently support this quintessence-like behavior, with the Statefinder trajectories approaching the $Λ$CDM fixed point in the far future. Overall, the power-law $f(Q)$ model provides a viable and competitive description of late-time cosmic acceleration.

</details>


### [146] [Dark-Sector Effects on the Phase Structure of Nonlinear Magnetic AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01695)
*Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,J. R. Villanueva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了完美流体暗物质(PFDM)和暗能量场对非线性磁荷AdS黑洞相结构的影响，发现SBH/LBH相变由非线性磁扇区驱动，PFDM参数λ会抑制相变并阻止规则黑洞核心形成，暗能量场在quintessence区域增强一级相变而在phantom区域仅表现出旋节线行为，GTD曲率奇点再现了相结构且临界标度律呈现普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨暗物质和暗能量等暗扇区对AdS黑洞热力学相结构的影响——特别是它们如何调制非线性电磁场驱动的SBH/LBH相变，以及这些效应是否具有普适的热力学特征。

Method: 在含非线性磁荷的AdS黑洞背景下引入PFDM和暗能量场（分别考虑quintessence和phantom两种状态方程参数区域），通过热力学分析研究相变行为，利用Gibbs构造和潜热刻画一级相变，并通过GTD的曲率奇点和临界标度幂律分析验证热力学几何结构。

Result: PFDM参数λ增大抑制SBH/LBH相变并使系统趋向单相区，λ非零阻止规则黑洞核心形成；quintessence区的暗能量增强一级相变（增大Gibbs构造面积和潜热、缩小临界区）；phantom区仅出现旋节线行为而无相共存；GTD曲率奇点与相结构一致，临界标度幂律与黑洞、宇宙学视界和真实流体吻合。

Conclusion: 暗扇区的存在不产生额外相变而是调制非线性磁扇区驱动的相变，其具体效应取决于暗能量状态方程参数；GTD分析支持黑洞热力学相变与真实流体之间存在普适的临界行为。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and a dark-energy field on the phase structure of a nonlinear magnetically charged Anti--de Sitter (AdS) black hole. We find that the small--large black hole (SBH/LBH) phase transition is driven by the nonlinear magnetic sector, while the dark sector does not generate additional transitions. In particular, increasing the magnitude of the PFDM parameter $λ$ suppresses this phase transition, eventually driving the system toward a single-phase regime. Moreover, a nonzero $λ$ prevents the formation of a regular black hole core. The effect of the dark-energy field depends on its equation-of-state parameter: in the quintessence regime, increasing its intensity strengthens the first-order phase transition, increasing the Gibbs construction area and latent heat while reducing the critical regime; in the phantom regime, the system exhibits spinodal behavior without phase coexistence. Finally, geometrothermodynamics (GTD) shows that the curvature singularities of the thermodynamic equilibrium manifold reproduce the phase structure of the black hole, while the associated power-law scaling near criticality is consistent with previous results for black holes, cosmological horizons, and real fluids, suggesting a universal thermodynamic behavior across these systems.

</details>


### [147] [Geodesics and thermodynamics of a $κ$-deformed anti-de Sitter black hole surrounded by a quintessence field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01704)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Izzet Sakalli,Erdem Sucu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kiselev五重态场中κ-变形的Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter黑洞的测地线结构和热力学性质。该变形由质量依赖的lapse函数产生，无需电荷或自旋即可形成内Cauchy视界，但无法消除中心奇点（Kretschmann标量以r^-10更强烈发散）。由于变形振幅与质量耦合，标准的Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律违反热力学第一定律，作者通过修正熵或重新标度质量来恢复第一定律并得到一致的Smarr关系。光学上，变形参数增大会缩小光子球和临界碰撞参数。作者还推导了闭式Joule-Thomson系数，发现κ-变形触发反转曲线（这在标准中性SAdS中不存在），且不产生van der Waals临界点，证明两种现象可干净解耦。变形将峰值Hawking辐射抑制约4倍并增加通量稀疏性，主要由温度下降驱动而非阴影变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索κ-变形时空在无电荷/无自旋条件下的黑洞物理，理解变形如何影响视界结构、热力学一致性、光学性质和热力学相变行为，特别是Joule-Thomson反转与van der Waals临界性之间可能存在的解耦。

Method: 采用质量依赖的lapse函数变形的κ-变形Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter黑洞模型，置于Kiselev五重态场背景中；分析测地线结构（光子球、临界碰撞参数）与Kretschmann奇异性；通过修正熵公式或质量重新标度恢复热力学第一定律并导出Smarr关系；计算闭式Joule-Thomson系数与反转曲线；分析Hawking辐射峰值与通量稀疏性。

Result: 1) 变形产生内Cauchy视界但不消除奇点，奇异性反而更强（r^-10发散）；2) 面积定律违反第一定律，通过修改熵或重标度质量可恢复，并获得一致Smarr关系；3) 变形参数增大使光子球和临界碰撞参数收缩；4) κ-变形在不产生van der Waals临界点的情况下触发Joule-Thomson反转曲线，实现两者解耦；5) 变形将峰值Hawking发射抑制约4倍并增强通量稀疏性，主要由温度下降驱动。

Conclusion: κ-变形为SAdS黑洞提供了非电荷/自旋依赖的Cauchy视界机制，但代价是更强的奇点发散；热力学一致性要求修正熵或质量定义；最重要的发现是Joule-Thomson反转与van der Waals临界性在此模型中明确解耦，为区分黑洞相变机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and thermodynamics of a $κ$-deformed Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter black hole in a Kiselev quintessence field, where dynamics are governed by a mass-dependent lapse deformation. This term produces an inner Cauchy horizon without requiring charge or spin, yet it fails to resolve the central singularity; instead, the Kretschmann scalar diverges more strongly as $r^{-10}$. Because the deformation amplitude couples to the mass, the standard Bekenstein-Hawking area law violates the first law of thermodynamics. We resolve this by either deriving a modified entropy or rescaling the mass, both of which restore the first law and yield a consistent Smarr relation. Optically, an increasing deformation parameter shrinks both the photon sphere and the critical impact parameter. We also derive a closed-form Joule-Thomson coefficient, revealing that the $κ$-deformation triggers an inversion curve a feature absent in standard neutral SAdS. Notably, this occurs without generating a van der Waals critical point, demonstrating that Joule-Thomson inversion and van der Waals criticality can be cleanly decoupled. Finally, the deformation suppresses peak Hawking emission by a factor of roughly four and increases flux sparsity, an effect driven almost entirely by a drop in temperature rather than changes to the black hole shadow.

</details>


### [148] [One-cycle radiation-reaction Magnusian for eccentric binaries through relative 1PN order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01713)
*Andrea Placidi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了非自旋紧致双星在平面有界偏心轨道上的一圈（一阶）Magnusian，达到相对一阶后牛顿（1PN）辐射反应线性阶，并构造了相应的近星点返回映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力波天文学中，理解偏心轨道的耗散动力学对于精确建模合并前双星系统的引力波发射至关重要。Magnus展开为处理非自治动力系统提供了一种系统方法，但此前尚未将其应用于偏心轨道上的紧致双星微扰动力学。

Method: 结合守恒1PN动力学及其Jacobi输运，与2.5PN和3.5PN辐射反力，在0<e<1范围内以闭合形式获得所有四个Magnusian分量（不展开偏心率）。利用Iyer-Will辐射反力规范参数的相关性分析，将影响化简为周期上边界并验证其在一个径向周期上的抵消。分别构造严格一阶映射和部分指数化映射，并比较两者在返回时间和进动相位上的累积残差。

Result: 结果复现了平均能量和角动量损失、其圆轨道极限以及1PN径向轨道元的长期演化。在固定近星点指数下，严格映射在40个径向周期的代表性演化中给出更小的残差，而部分指数化使累积近星点计时残差降低约一个数量级。在等质量的一周期网格上，部分指数化在每个采样点均改善了径向作用和能量更新。

Conclusion: 严格一阶映射与部分指数化映射的比较是依赖可观测量而异的：前者在相空间预报残差上更优，后者在周期累积计时残差上显著更优，表明部分指数化在长时标演化中具有实用优势。

Abstract: We derive the one-cycle first Magnusian for nonspinning compact binaries on planar, bound eccentric orbits through relative first post-Newtonian (1PN) order and linear order in radiation reaction, and construct the associated first-order periastron return map. Combining the conservative 1PN dynamics and its Jacobi transport with the 2.5PN and 3.5PN radiation-reaction forces, we obtain all four components in closed form for \(0<e<1\), without expanding in eccentricity. The dependence on all eight Iyer--Will radiation-reaction gauge parameters reduces to periodic coboundaries and therefore cancels over a radial cycle. The result reproduces the averaged energy and angular-momentum losses, their circular limit, and the secular evolution of the 1PN radial orbital elements. The two remaining components encode the dissipative angle-sector information. Projecting the resulting phase-space update onto the perturbed periastron return section yields the corrections to the return time and apsidal phase. We then compare the strict first-order map with a partially exponentiated map built from the same Magnusian. The comparison is observable dependent: at fixed periastron index, the strict map gives smaller residuals for a representative evolution over 40 radial cycles, whereas partial exponentiation reduces the accumulated periastron-timing residual by about an order of magnitude. Across a representative one-cycle grid at equal masses, partial exponentiation improves the radial-action and energy updates at every sampled point.

</details>


### [149] [How Much Geometry Does Horizon Entropy Determine? A Discourse on Entropy, Gravity, and Observable Quantities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01720)
*Yen Chin Ong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Jacobson热力学推导爱因斯坦场方程的基础上，系统比较了"广义熵与变G"（GEVAG）框架和"熵-引力对应"（EGC）框架两种将广义视界熵引入时空热力学与几何的方法。两者共享相同的视界一致性关系，但在全局因果结构、渐近几何、多中心配置下的径向处方等方面存在本质差异。文章还探讨了GEVAG的近视界扩展如何恢复热力学质量与ADM质量的等价性，并分析了不同处方对Bekenstein界的影响，发现Bekenstein界在近视界GEVAG、EGC和基于Wald熵的f(R)重构方法之间提供了意想不到的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobson的热力学推导建立了一致性关系，但纳入广义视界熵（如f(A)形式的熵）时，不同的实现路径（GEVAG与EGC）可能产生不同的物理后果。作者旨在澄清这些方法在概念和物理上的相似性与本质分歧，特别是它们对时空几何全局结构的不同影响，以及由此引出的更深层问题：实验室中实际测量的引力常数G到底是什么。

Method: 采用理论分析与比较研究的方法：(1) 系统对比GEVAG（仅视界处有效耦合G_eff=G/f'(A)）与EGC（将视界关系推广为径向变化的体耦合G_eff(r)）两种框架；(2) 分析多中心构型下EGC径向处方的歧义性；(3) 提出GEVAG的近视界扩展以修复热力学质量与ADM质量的等价性；(4) 通过Bekenstein界检验不同处方，并与基于Wald熵的f(R)重构方法进行交叉比较。

Result: GEVAG与EGC虽共享视界一致性条件，但两者的全局因果结构和渐近几何可能差异显著；EGC的径向处方在多中心构型下引入额外歧义；近视界扩展版GEVAG成功恢复了热力学与ADM质量的等价性；Bekenstein界揭示了近视界GEVAG、EGC与f(R)重构方法中得到的G_eff之间存在一个出人意料的统一联系。

Conclusion: 不同的广义熵引力处方虽然在视界处看似一致，但在更大尺度上可能预测截然不同的时空结构。Bekenstein界可作为约束不同处方的重要物理判据，并暗示这些方法在近视界区域存在深层统一性。文章最终提出核心开放问题：需要明确实验室中测量的引力常数G与这些理论中的有效耦合G_eff之间的关系。

Abstract: Building on Jacobson's thermodynamics derivation of Einstein field equations, the "generalized entropy and varying-G" (GEVAG) framework was proposed to consistently incorporate generalized horizon entropy $S=f(A)/4G$ into both the thermodynamics and the geometry of spacetime. This leads to an effective gravitational coupling $G_\text{eff}=G/f'(A)$. A related "entropy-gravity correspondence" (EGC) instead is equivalent to promoting this horizon relation to a radially varying bulk coupling $G_\text{eff}(r)$. We clarify the similarities and fundamental distinctions between these approaches. Although they share the same horizon consistency relation, their global causal structures and asymptotic geometries can differ substantially. In addition, the radial prescription of EGC introduces an additional ambiguity for multi-center configurations. We further examine a near-horizon extension of GEVAG, which restores the equivalence between thermodynamic and ADM masses that is absent in the horizon-only formulation. We then discuss the implications of these different prescriptions for the Bekenstein bound. The Bekenstein bound provides a surprising link between $G_\text{eff}$ in near horizon GEVAG, EGC, and the one obtained in the recent $f(R)$-reconstruction approach of generalized entropy geometry via Wald entropy. Finally, we raise the most important question: what exactly is the $G$ measured in laboratories?

</details>


### [150] [Exploring the construction of field equations for regular black holes with dynamical regularization scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01733)
*Raúl Carballo-Rubio,Jacopo Mazza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次探讨了将正则化尺度作为动力学场的黑洞场方程构造问题，并证明该问题可归结为求解标量场与度规自由度之间耦合函数的线性偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞动力学是新兴研究领域，已有工作通过引入新物理尺度作为调节器来修正爱因斯坦场方程以正则化球对称黑洞解，但此前还没有研究将正则化尺度本身视为动力学场的构造问题。

Method: 讨论了球对称引力耦合标量场的一般场方程族，将正则化尺度提升为动力学场，考察恒定标量场扇区是否包含描述静态规则黑洞的解（无需额外物理尺度）；将该问题约化求解标量场与度规自由度之间耦合函数的线性偏微分方程。

Result: 证明了关于是否存在静态规则黑洞解（仅依赖标量场提供的尺度）的问题可以归结为求解一个关于耦合函数的线性偏微分方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为规则黑洞核心的演化和替代动力学解的构造提供了新颖途径，可能为物质反作用导致的规则黑洞核心演化提供有效描述。

Abstract: The study of the dynamics of regular black holes is an emergent area of research. Recent works have discussed how to deform the Einstein field equations to regularize spherically symmetric black hole solutions due to the introduction of a new physics scale acting as regulator. In this exploratory paper, we consider for the first time the problem of constructing field equations in which the regularization scale is a dynamical field. Such field equations may provide an effective description of the evolution of the cores of regular black holes due to the backreaction of matter, and a novel avenue for constructing alternative dynamical solutions of theoretical and phenomenological interest. We discuss a general family of field equations describing spherically symmetric gravity coupled to a scalar field, and study whether the sector with a constant scalar field contains solutions describing static regular black holes with no additional physics scales aside from the ones provided by the scalar field. We show that this question can be reduced to solving a linear partial differential equation for the coupling functions between the scalar and the metric degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [151] [Exploration of Zero-Complexity Compact Stars in Higher Dimensions under the Finch-Skea Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01803)
*Shyam Das,Megandhren Govender,Kevin Reddy,Bikram Keshari Parida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在(n+2)维爱因斯坦引力框架下，基于Finch-Skea几何构造了满足零复杂度条件的精确紧致星模型，获得了描述各向异性流体球的新一类精确内部解，并系统分析了其物理性质及额外维度对结构特征的影响。这些解是广义复杂度框架中首个精确的高维Finch-Skea紧致星模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到Herrera引力复杂度概念向任意高维时空拓展的启发，作者希望在Finch-Skea几何背景下构造满足消失复杂度条件的精确紧致星模型，从而将零复杂度恒星结构从四维广义相对论自然推广到高维引力中。

Method: 将高维爱因斯坦场方程与广义复杂度形式相结合，在(n+2)维爱因斯坦引力的Finch-Skea几何中求解，获得描述零复杂度各向异性流体球的新一类精确内部解。通过分析物质变量、压力各向异性和平衡条件的动力学行为，以及正则性、能量条件、因果性和稳定性的标准要求来评估模型物理性质，并进一步考察时空维度对恒星结构特征的影响。

Result: 成功构造了满足零复杂度条件的高维Finch-Skea紧致星精确解。研究表明额外维度的存在会显著改变内部物质分布，但同时保持了模型的物理可行性。这些解是广义复杂度框架下首个精确的高维Finch-Skea紧致星模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为四维广义相对论之外的零复杂度恒星结构提供了自然的高维拓展，并为在高维引力中研究自引力系统提供了稳健的理论框架。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent extension of Herrera's gravitational complexity to arbitrary higher-dimensional spacetimes, we investigate exact compact-star models that satisfy the vanishing-complexity condition within the Finch--Skea geometry in $(n+2)$-dimensional Einstein gravity. By combining the higher-dimensional Einstein field equations with the generalized complexity formalism, we obtain a new class of exact interior solutions describing anisotropic fluid spheres with zero complexity. The physical properties of these models are evaluated by analyzing the behavior of the matter variables, pressure anisotropy, and equilibrium conditions, alongside the standard requirements of regularity, energy conditions, causality, and stability. We further explore the influence of spacetime dimensionality on the structural characteristics of the stellar configurations, demonstrating that the presence of extra dimensions significantly modifies the internal matter distribution while preserving physical viability. The solutions presented here represent the first exact higher-dimensional Finch--Skea compact-star models constructed within this recently developed generalized complexity framework \cite{choudas}. These results provide a natural extension of zero-complexity stellar configurations beyond four-dimensional General Relativity and offer a robust framework for investigating self-gravitating systems in higher- dimensional gravity.

</details>


### [152] [Scalar Field Model for Dark Matter Spikes Surrounding Sgr A$^*$ and M87$^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01900)
*Ramin G. Daghigh,Michael D. Green,Gabor Kunstatter*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向工程技术，利用具有非标准势能的实际标量场来模拟黑洞周围的暗物质尖峰结构，使所得几何可从引力波和阴影观测中检验，并允许包含斯派克反作用的引力微扰研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论模型预言黑洞在暗物质中绝热增长会在事件视界附近形成高密度暗物质"尖峰"，其密度呈幂律分布ρ(r)∝r^{-γ_sp}。该尖峰可能对引力波和黑洞阴影半径产生可观测影响，因此需要建立一个具有协变作用量基础的、物理上自洽的暗物质尖峰模型。

Method: 作者使用实标量场配合非标准势能来模拟暗物质尖峰。具体方法是"逆向工程"运动方程——即先设定所需的能量密度（幂律形式）和合理的背景几何，再反推求解出能够允许该解的标量场势能。

Result: 所得几何是可检验的。由于该解源于协变作用量，使得可以在包含尖峰反作用的条件下研究黑洞周围的引力微扰。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种从协变作用量出发构建暗物质尖峰模型的新方法，为后续通过引力波和阴影观测检验暗物质尖峰效应，以及系统研究包含反作用效应的引力微扰铺平了道路。

Abstract: Theoretical models suggest that the adiabatic growth of a black hole immersed in dark matter can lead to the formation of high density regions of dark matter, known as ``spikes'', near the black hole event horizon. The density of these spikes is determined theoretically and observationally to be a power law of the form $ρ(r) \propto r^{-γ_\text{sp}}$. It has been shown that the spike can potentially have a detectable impact on the emitted gravitational waves and shadow radius of the central black hole. In this work, we model the dark matter spike using a real scalar field with a non-standard potential. More specifically we ``reverse engineer'' the equations of motion to find a potential for the scalar field that permits a solution to the equations of motion with desired energy density and reasonable background geometry. We show that the emerging geometry is testable. In addition, the fact that the solution is derived from a covariant action makes it possible to study gravitational perturbations of the black hole in the presence of a spike including the backreaction of the spike.

</details>


### [153] [A Tale of Two Calibrators: Comparing Newtonian and Photon Calibration in Advanced LIGO after the Third Observing Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01917)
*Dana Jones,Michael P. Ross,Ling Sun,Jeffrey S. Kissel,Dripta Bhattacharjee,Anthony Sanchez,Bram J. J. Slagmolen,Jens Gundlach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对LIGO引力波探测器的两种独立直接力标定方法（牛顿标定器NCal和光子标定器PCal）进行了对比分析。在LIGO Hanford天文台第三次观测运行结束后进行了一系列直接力注入实验，发现在30 Hz频带内每个注入频率上，NCal和PCal的实测/期望应变振幅比值差异约为1%，表明两系统之间存在微小系统性偏差，凸显了维持多个独立标定参考的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIGO应变读数的精确标定对从引力波探测中提取信息至关重要。目前LIGO仅依赖PCal系统作为绝对标定参考，缺少独立验证手段，因此需要对NCal和PCal两种直接力标定系统进行比较评估，以验证标定的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 在LIGO Hanford天文台第三次观测运行结束后，使用NCal和PCal系统执行一系列直接力注入实验。对每次注入计算实测应变振幅与期望应变振幅的比值，并在30 Hz频带内对各注入频率下两种系统的比值进行对比分析。

Result: 在30 Hz频带内的每个注入频率上，NCal与PCal的实测/期望应变振幅比值差异约为1%，表明两个独立标定系统之间存在小的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 两种标定系统间约1%的系统性差异凸显了维持多个独立标定参考对验证LIGO标定准确性的重要性，仅依赖单一PCal系统作为绝对标定参考可能存在未被察觉的系统性误差风险。

Abstract: Precise calibration of LIGO's strain readout plays a vital role in our ability to extract information from gravitational-wave detections. We present a comparative analysis of two independent direct-force calibration methods: the Newtonian Calibrator (NCal) and Photon Calibrator (PCal) systems. Currently, LIGO relies solely on the PCal system as its absolute calibration reference. To facilitate comparison, a series of direct-force injections were performed using the NCal and PCal systems at the LIGO Hanford observatory just after the completion of the third observing run. We compute the ratio of the measured to expected strain amplitudes for every injection. For each injection frequency across a 30 Hz band, the ratios of NCal and PCal differ by $\sim$1%, demonstrating a small systematic discrepancy between the two systems. This difference highlights the importance of maintaining multiple independent calibration references to validate LIGO's calibration.

</details>


### [154] [Collective Onset of Matter-Induced Scalarization around a Black Hole with Two Thin Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02066)
*Masahiro Kaminaga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究引力的静态黑洞被两个分离的薄物质壳层包围时，物质诱导标量化的线性起始条件。推导出有限秩的临界条件，表明两个单独亚临界的壳层可以共同使无标量黑洞失稳，并给出临界标量云的闭合形式和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究物质诱导标量化在多个薄物质壳层存在时的线性起始行为，特别是探究多个单独亚临界壳层能否合作产生集体失稳效应，以及精确的Israel壳层轨道对标量化起始的影响。

Method: 采用标量微扰与度规和物质微扰在一阶解耦的设定，分析静态球形黑洞外部区域，将起始问题归结为有限秩条件1-a1-a2+(1-χ)a1a2=0。通过解析推导临界标量云的闭合形式，并对Schwarzschild探测问题和三区域Israel壳层背景分别求解静态起始条件。

Result: 导出了两壳层系统的有限秩临界条件，证明两个单独亚临界壳层可共同引发失稳。数值验证了超阈值时会出现有限增长率的增长模式。对精确的Israel壳层背景也推导了静态起始条件，并发现Israel迹的符号转变，其光壳极限出现在Schwarzschild光子球处。

Conclusion: 两个空间分离的薄物质壳层即使各自亚临界也能通过集体效应使无标量黑洞失稳，且壳层间距（通过χ=𝒮(R2)/𝒮(R1)反映）对此有决定性影响。Israel迹的符号转变与光子球位置的联系暗示了壳层几何效应对标量化的调节作用。

Abstract: We study the linear onset of matter-induced scalarization for a static black hole surrounded by two spatially separated thin matter shells. We assume that the conformal matter coupling is unity on the scalar-free background and that its logarithmic derivative vanishes there. The scalar perturbation then decouples from the metric and matter perturbations at first order, while the invariant surface traces of the shells give singular terms in the scalar equation.
  For a static spherical black-hole exterior with no bulk scalar effective-mass term between the shells, the onset reduces to the finite-rank condition $$ 1-a_1-a_2+(1-χ)a_1a_2=0, \qquad χ=\frac{{\cal S}(R_2)}{{\cal S}(R_1)}, $$ where $a_j$ is the attractive strength of shell $j$ normalized by its one--shell threshold on the same background and ${\cal S}$ is the static radial resistance. This relation shows that two individually subcritical shells can collectively destabilize the scalar-free black hole.
  We derive the critical scalar cloud in closed form and verify numerically, in the Schwarzschild probe problem, that a growing mode with a finite growth rate appears beyond the threshold. For an exact scalar-free background consisting of three Schwarzschild regions joined by two Israel shells, we separately derive the static onset condition and the corresponding critical cloud. The exact Israel trace also exhibits a sign transition whose light-shell limit occurs at the Schwarzschild photon sphere.

</details>


### [155] [Cosmological implications for hairy black holes via spontaneous symmetry breaking: Are Hairy Black Holes Primordial?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02102)
*Suruj Jyoti Das,Miok Park*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Einstein-Scalar-Gauss-Bonnet（ESGB）理论中，由自发对称性破缺产生的带毛黑洞是否能与宇宙学演化相容。研究发现，由于GB项在宇宙学尺度上的时间依赖性，标量场在FLRW背景中的动力学与静态黑洞背景中具有本质差异。在暴胀期间对称性破缺真空存在，但暴胀后减速膨胀使GB项变号，导致有效势短暂无下界；随后GB贡献衰减，标量自相互作用恢复对称性。由此推导出对耦合强度、截断尺度和黑洞质量的严格约束，最终发现只有质量约为数克的超轻黑洞（即带毛原初黑洞）才能形成标量毛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答一个关键问题：在ESGB理论中通过自发对称性破缺生成的黑洞标量毛，是否能与我们所处的宇宙学演化和谐共存，从而为原初黑洞等物理场景提供理论基础。

Method: 引入具有标量自相互作用的ESGB理论，该自相互作用在宇宙学尺度上显著而在黑洞附近可忽略。基于GB项在FLRW背景与静态黑洞背景中行为的不同，分析有效势在各演化阶段（暴胀期、减速膨胀期、后期）的演变，并考察无界相期间标量场扰动的tachyonic放大效应。

Result: 推导出耦合强度、截断尺度及黑洞质量的严格约束条件，主要来源于避免无界相期间标量场扰动的高效tachyonic放大。在相容的参数区域内，仅质量约为数克的超轻黑洞（即带毛原初黑洞）能够形成标量毛。

Conclusion: ESGB理论中的带毛黑洞与宇宙学演化相容性极为有限——普通恒星质量黑洞无法形成毛，只有极轻的原初黑洞才可能是带毛的宏大质量候选体。这为区分带毛黑洞理论提供了可检验的宇宙学预测。

Abstract: We investigate whether hairy black holes generated through spontaneous symmetry breaking in Einstein-Scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (ESGB) theory, involving a complex scalar field with a global $U(1)$ symmetry, can be compatible with cosmological evolution. To this end, we introduce the ESGB theory with a scalar self-interaction that becomes relevant on cosmological scales while remaining negligible near the black hole. Owing to the time dependence of the GB term on cosmological scales, the scalar field dynamics in the evolving FLRW background differ qualitatively from those in the nearly static black hole background. In particular, for scalar-GB couplings compatible with hairy black hole formation, the effective potential supports a symmetry-broken vacuum throughout inflation. However, after inflation, a decelerated expansion changes the sign of the GB term, temporarily making the effective potential unbounded from below. As the GB contribution subsequently decreases, the scalar self-interaction eventually dominates and restores the symmetry. Within this schematic framework, we derive stringent constraints on the coupling strengths, the cutoff scale, and the black hole mass, which primarily arise for avoiding efficient tachyonic amplification of the scalar field perturbations during the unbounded phase. For cutoff scales compatible with both cosmological evolution and scalar hair formation, we find that only ultralight black holes with masses of the order of a few grams can develop scalar hair, identifying them as hairy primordial black holes.

</details>


### [156] [Accretion dynamics of thin and thick disks in charged Kalb-Ramond black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02223)
*Ednaldo L. B. Junior,José Tarciso S. S. Junior,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Jorde A. A. Ramos,Manuel E. Rodrigues,Diego Rubiera-Garcia,Luís F. Dias da Silva,Henrique A. Vieira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond（KR）引力中带电静态球对称黑洞的吸积性质。通过将阴影半径与EHT对人马座A*的观测约束对比，确定了电荷Q和KR参数l的参数空间（-0.3900 ≤ l ≤ 0.1898），并发现电荷引入导致Q-l简并，部分兼容区域延伸至裸奇点扇区。在约束内研究了几何薄盘的时间平均能量通量、温度分布、发射谱和辐射效率（较高l增强辐射输出，较低l抑制），以及几何厚环面的轨道半径、平衡构型和流体拓扑等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索KR引力中自发洛伦兹对称性破缺（由参数l刻画）以及电荷对黑洞吸积性质与观测约束的联合影响，以提供强引力区域KR引力的潜在可观测信号。

Method: 首先通过EHT对Sgr A*阴影半径的观测约束限定(Q,l)参数空间；随后在约束范围内，对几何薄吸积盘计算时间平均能量通量、温度分布、发射谱、时间平均力矩和质辐射换效率；对几何厚、恒定角动量环面形吸积盘研究洛伦兹对称性破缺对轨道半径、径向范围、平衡环面区间及流体拓扑的影响。

Result: 获得宽泛约束-0.3900 ≤ l ≤ 0.1898，其中部分兼容参数空间延伸至裸奇点扇区；电荷增强了参数简并并放大了l对辐射输出的影响；薄盘辐射输出随l增大而增强；厚盘方面，l使特征轨道半径偏移、平衡构型径向范围压缩、有限平衡环面区间变窄，并使平衡流体拓扑向外开放构型转移。

Conclusion: 电荷与自发洛伦兹对称性破缺共同影响黑洞吸积流的观测约束及结构与发射性质，为KR引力在强引力区域提供潜在可观测特征信号。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the accretion properties of an electrically charged, static, spherically symmetric black hole in Kalb-Ramond (KR) gravity, where a dimensionless parameter $l$ characterizes spontaneous Lorentz symmetry-breaking. We first constrain the free charge and KR parameter space, $(Q,l)$, by comparing the predicted shadow radius of this family of solutions with the bounds inferred from Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sagittarius A*. The inclusion of electric charge introduces a degeneracy between $Q$ and $l$, enlarging the region compatible with the observed shadow size and yielding the broad constraint $-0.3900 \leq l \leq 0.1898$, with part of the compatible parameter space extending into the naked-singularity sector. We then investigate relativistic accretion disks around the charged KR black hole within the above constraints. For geometrically thin disks, we compute the time averaged energy flux, temperature distribution, emission spectrum, time averaged torque, and mass-to-radiation conversion efficiency, finding that the radiative output increases for higher values of $l$, while lower values of $l$ suppress it. Furthermore, when coupled with the electric charge this effect is amplified. For geometrically thick, constant-angular momentum tori-shaped disks, Lorentz symmetry-breaking shifts the characteristic orbital radii, compresses the radial extent of equilibrium configurations, narrows the interval supporting finite equilibrium tori, and shifts the stationary-fluid topology toward open and unconfined configurations outside that interval. These results show how charge and spontaneous Lorentz symmetry-breaking jointly affect observational constraints and the structure and emission properties of black hole accretion flows, providing potential signatures of KR gravity in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [157] [Boundary-Geometry-Driven Black Hole Formation in Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02311)
*Dipanjan Dey,Puskar Mondal,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文识别出一种由边界几何驱动的机制，用于在真空广义相对论中动态形成边缘外俘获面（MOTS）。温和的三维各向异性在致密柯西域内演化，边界广义平均曲率在收缩或膨胀期间增大，驱动边界跨越Yau几何阈值，从而从未俘获的初值数据强制形成MOTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOTS形成结果多依赖短脉冲（short-pulse）机制来集中引力辐射，本文旨在提供一种纯粹真空物理的、不依赖短脉冲集中机制的形成路径，展示全局几何效应也能驱动MOTS形成。

Method: 通过分析致密柯西域中温和三维各向异性的演化，控制有效各向同性的厚度，使边界广义平均曲率在边界收缩或扩张过程中增大，从而跨越Yau几何阈值导致MOTS形成。

Result: 证明了从初始未俘获的数据出发，仅通过边界几何效应即可强制形成MOTS，并进一步将文献中的特征剪切构造解释为同一各向异性真空动力学的零（null）表现。

Conclusion: 给出了一种不依赖短脉冲机制的纯真空物理实现，表明MOTS形成可通过全局几何效应达成，为黑洞视界形成的几何动力学提供了新的理解途径。

Abstract: We identify a boundary-geometry-driven mechanism for the dynamical formation of marginally outer trapped surfaces (MOTSs) in vacuum general relativity. Mild three-dimensional anisotropies evolve inside a compact Cauchy domain whose effective isotropic thickness remains controlled, while the generalized boundary mean curvature can increase during either contracting or expanding boundary evolution. This drives the boundary across Yau's geometric threshold, forcing MOTS formation from initially untrapped data. We further interpret the characteristic shear construction of Ref.~\cite{MondalYau2026} as the null manifestation of the same anisotropic vacuum dynamics. The result provides a purely vacuum physical realization of MOTS formation through global geometric effects, without invoking a short-pulse concentration mechanism for gravitational radiation.

</details>


### [158] [Redshift Spectroscopy as a Probe of Regular Black Holes, Black Bounces, and Scalar-Hair Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02313)
*Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig,Joel Saavedra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Phys. Rev. D. 107, 064019 (2023)的基础上，发展了一个统一且模型无关的框架，用于分析一般静态、球对称时空中光子频率移的光谱学。该框架扩展到包含源的本动速度和冷等离子体色散效应，并推广到Schwarzschild几何周围的二阶微扰展开，适用于各种变形致密天体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中光子频率移的研究多针对特定时空模型分别处理，缺乏一个统一、模型无关的理论框架来系统描述静态球对称时空中的光谱学特征，特别是跨越正规黑洞、黑洞弹跳、虫洞和标量场支撑的无视界时空等不同致密天体模型。

Method: 作者采用三个任意径向度规函数表征的线元，推导了有质量与无质量探针的守恒量精确表达式、圆类时测地线条件、依赖局域发射角的光子碰撞参数，以及远处静态观测者测得的局域红移和蓝移分支。框架进一步推广至包含源的本动速度和非磁化冷等离子体中的光子传播，并基于Schwarzschild几何构建了无量纲变形参数的二阶微扰展开。

Result: 得到了轨道能量、角动量、发射体四速度、光子碰撞参数以及真空和等离子体频移的显式二阶修正表达式。在真空情况下，证明轨道光谱学中的几何结构与光子球和阴影碰撞参数由相同的外部零临界轨道决定。将该形式应用于非线性电动力学的正规黑洞、Simpson-Visser黑洞弹跳时空中和Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour-Wyman几何。

Conclusion: 该框架表明统一的局域光谱学语言可以适用于正规黑洞、黑洞弹跳、虫洞以及标量场支撑的无视界致密天体等多种模型，为变形致密天体的光谱观测提供了一个通用的理论工具。

Abstract: Motivated by [Phys. Rev. D. 107, 064019 (2023)], we develop a unified and model-independent framework for the spectroscopy of photon frequency shifts in generic static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. Working with a line element characterized by three arbitrary radial metric functions, we derive exact expressions for the conserved quantities of massive and massless probes, the conditions for circular timelike geodesics, the local-emission-angle-dependent photon impact parameter, and the corresponding local redshift and blueshift branches measured by distant static observers. The formalism is further extended to include the line-of-sight peculiar motion of the source and the local propagation of photons in a nonmagnetized cold plasma, thereby identifying the gravitational, orbital, and dispersive factors entering the frequency-shift signal under the stated assumptions. We also show that, in vacuum, the same geometric structures governing orbital spectroscopy determine the photon sphere and the shadow impact parameter whenever an external null critical orbit is present. To make the framework suitable for deformed compact-object models, we construct a perturbative expansion around Schwarzschild geometry up to second order in a dimensionless deformation parameter, obtaining explicit corrections to the orbital energy, angular momentum, emitter four-velocity, photon impact parameter, and the vacuum and plasma frequency shifts. We apply the formalism to regular black holes from nonlinear electrodynamics, the Simpson--Visser black-bounce spacetime, and the Fisher--Janis--Newman--Winicour--Wyman geometry. These examples show that the same local spectroscopic language can be used across regular-black-hole, black-bounce, wormhole, and scalar-supported horizonless sectors.

</details>


### [159] [The Analytical Solutions of Dyonic Black Holes in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02380)
*Haoyuan Luo,Nan Cao,Xiao Yan Chew,Kok-Geng Lim,Chao Chen,Dong-han Yeom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein–Euler–Heisenberg理论中，针对满足2b > a（无需限定b = a/2）的非线性耦合常数，构造了精确的解析dionic（兼具电与磁）黑洞解，质量函数以Lauricella超几何函数的闭式形式表达，并恢复先前文献的特例结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前该理论中纯电和纯磁黑洞解可解析构造，但对应的dionic（电-磁混合）解仅通过数值方法获得；近期在特殊耦合点b = a/2上实现了解析构造。作者希望将这一解析构造推广到更一般的耦合格局，突破b = a/2的限制。

Method: 作者对一般的dionic扇区进行重新审视，证明当非线性耦合常数满足条件2b > a时，无需施加限制b = a/2，即可解析构造精确的dionic黑洞解。质量函数以Lauricella超几何函数的闭式形式给出。通过取极限2b → a，恢复文献[Ahmed:2026ufj]中在特殊耦合b = a/2下的已知解。

Result: 成功构造了满足2b > a条件下的一般解析dionic黑洞解；Euler–Heisenberg耦合b = 7a/4时也获得了精确的dionic解；当2b → a的极限情形下，可恢复文献中基于特殊耦合点的已知解。

Conclusion: 该工作将解析dionic黑洞解的适用范围从单一特殊耦合点b = a/2推广到更一般的2b > a区域，显著拓展了Einstein–Euler–Heisenberg理论中可解析求解的dionic黑洞解的族类，为后续深入研究这些解的物理性质与热力学提供了基础。

Abstract: Recently, we constructed analytical purely electric and purely magnetic black-hole solutions in Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg theory, while the corresponding dyonic solutions were obtained numerically \cite{Luo:2026srx}. Motivated by the recent analytical construction of a dyonic black hole at the special coupling locus $b=a/2$ \cite{Ahmed:2026ufj}, we revisit the general dyonic sector. We show that exact analytical dyonic black-hole solutions can be constructed for nonlinear couplings satisfying $2b>a$, without imposing the restriction $b=a/2$. The mass function is expressed in closed form in terms of the Lauricella hypergeometric function. In particular, our construction yields an exact dyonic solution for the Euler--Heisenberg coupling $b=7a/4$. The solution of Ref.~\cite{Ahmed:2026ufj} is recovered in the limiting case $2b\rightarrow a$.

</details>


### [160] [Horizon energy fluctuations beyond Einstein's gravity: Gauss-Bonnet, Lovelock and Quantum deformed frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02456)
*P. B. Krishna,Lini Devassy,Titus K. Mathew*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在正则系综框架下研究宇宙学视界的能量涨落，发现视界能量涨落在渐近德西特极限下稳定为常数值，该结果不依赖于具体引力理论，且对高阶曲率和量子修正具有鲁棒性。最终德西特态满足全息等分配条件，视界熵达到极大常数值，宇宙演化以热力学平衡态为终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙演化的热力学终点状态，探究德西特宇宙是否可以被刻画为热力学平衡态，以及不同引力理论下视界热力学行为是否具有普适性。

Method: 采用正则系综框架，将宇宙学视界视为由温度和熵表征的热力学系统，对(n+1)维爱因斯坦引力、Gauss-Bonnet引力、Lovelock引力及含量子修正熵模型分别分析视界能量涨落及其渐近行为。

Result: 视界能量涨落在渐近德西特极限下趋于由理论无关的常数值；该结果对高阶曲率修正和量子熵修正均保持稳定。渐近德西特宇宙满足全息等分配条件，视界熵达到极大常数值，类似普通宏观系统。

Conclusion: 熵极大化、全息等分配与能量涨落的抑制共同刻画了渐近德西特宇宙作为宇宙演化平衡终态的统一热力学图像，表明该结论在各类引力理论中具有普适性。

Abstract: We study thermal energy fluctuations of cosmological horizons within the canonical ensemble framework, treating the horizon as a thermodynamic system characterized by temperature and entropy. The analysis is performed for a class of gravitational theories, including (n+1)-dimensional Einstein gravity, Gauss-Bonnet gravity, Lovelock gravity, and models incorporating quantum deformed entropy corrections. We find that horizon energy fluctuations stabilize to a constant value in the asymptotic de Sitter limit, independent of the underlying gravity theory and robust against higher-curvature and quantum corrections. It is worth mentioning that in its final de Sitter state, the universe obeys holographic equipartition condition and in consequence the horizon entropy attains a maximum constant value, just like an ordinary macroscopic system. These findings establish a unified thermodynamic picture in which entropy maximization, holographic equipartition, and the suppression of energy fluctuations collectively characterize the asymptotic de Sitter universe as the equilibrium end state of cosmic evolution.

</details>


### [161] [Covariant linear response theory for a photon gas in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02615)
*Jianan Wang,Long Cui,Bin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了弯曲时空中光子气体的协变输运理论，从玻尔兹曼方程出发并采用弛豫时间近似，计算了二阶输运系数，并导出了菲克定律、傅里叶定律的广义相对论形式以及球对称吸积盘的辐射扩散方程，为强引力场中的辐射输运提供协变描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非相对论统计描述在强引力场中失效，需要建立适用于弯曲时空的光子气体协变输运理论，以满足高能天体物理中强引力场辐射输运的应用需求。

Method: 从弯曲时空中的玻尔兹曼方程出发，采用弛豫时间近似，计算至二阶的输运系数；利用一阶结果推导菲克定律和傅里叶定律的广义相对论推广形式，并求解球对称吸积盘的辐射扩散方程。

Result: 输运系数可计算至二阶，且不能视为有质量粒子结果的零质量极限，因为零质量粒子的相空间几何根本不同。在二阶，引力场使输运系数成为张量值并诱导横向流（类似于霍尔效应）。

Conclusion: 本文为强引力场中的辐射输运提供了协变描述，可应用于高能天体物理场景，例如球对称吸积盘的辐射扩散过程。

Abstract: To overcome the failure of the conventional non-relativistic description of statistics in strong gravitational fields, we develop a covariant theory for a photon gas in curved spacetime, starting from the Boltzmann equation and employing the relaxation time approximation. The transport coefficients are computed up to second order, which are not a massless limit of the massive-particle results due to the fundamentally different phase-space geometry of null particles. At second order, the gravitational field renders the coefficients tensor-valued and induces transverse fluxes, analogous to the Hall effect. Using the first-order results, we derive relativistic generalizations of Fick's and Fourier's laws and obtain the radiation diffusion equation for spherically symmetric accretion disks. This provides a covariant description of radiative transport in strong gravitational fields for high-energy astrophysical applications.

</details>


### [162] [Frequency-domain extended-effective-source gravitational self-force for eccentric Schwarzschild orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02635)
*Xuchen Lu,Yungui Gong,Bokai Zhang,Chao Zhang,Wenting Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造并实现了频率域扩展有效源（EES）公式，用于求解Schwarzschild时空中偏心轨道上的一阶Lorenz规范引力自力（GSF）。通过将穿刺源的内外分支解析延拓至整个振动区域，克服了径向振荡导致的谱收敛缓慢问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效源方法是引力自力计算中的实用途径，尤其在奇异延迟场无法逐模直接处理时。但此前该方法仅成功应用于Schwarzschild时空中圆轨道的频率域一阶计算，偏心轨道的情况尚未实现。

Method: 构建频率域的扩展有效源公式，将物理穿刺源和有效源在内外分支之间切换的问题，通过对两个分支在径向振动（libration）区域进行解析延拓来解决，进而得到光滑的扩展源，并求解耦合的Lorenz规范微扰方程组。

Result: 实现了偏心运动的端到端引力扩展有效源计算，验证了将频率域有效源计算推广至二阶所需的计算要素。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了有效源方法在偏心轨道GSF计算中的空白，为向二阶GSF计算的频率域扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Effective-source formulations provide a practical route to gravitational self-force (GSF) calculations when singular retarded fields cannot be handled directly mode by mode. Up to now, only frequency-domain first-order GSF for circular orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime was obtained with the effective-source method. We construct and implement a frequency-domain extended effective-source (EES) formulation for first-order Lorenz-gauge GSF on eccentric orbits in Schwarzschild spacetime. The central obstacle is that radial libration forces the physical puncture and effective source to switch between interior and exterior branches, limiting their differentiability and producing slow spectral convergence. We overcome this obstruction by analytically extending both branches across the libration region and solving the coupled Lorenz-gauge perturbation equations for the resulting smooth extended sources. This provides an end-to-end gravitational EES implementation for eccentric motion and validates a computational ingredient needed for extending frequency-domain effective-source calculations toward second order.

</details>


### [163] [Relativistic stellar collapse of initially static configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02655)
*Keshlan S. Govinder,Megandhren Govender,Sunil Maharaj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了存在电磁场和宇宙学常数时辐射相对论性恒星的动力学行为，利用相平面分析方法研究控制其时间演化的非线性二阶方程，发现一般物理参数条件下会出现与非零电荷和宇宙学常数相关的折叠分岔，从而更全面地理解了无剪切构型在引力坍缩过程中的时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清此前关于辐射相对论恒星在电磁场和宇宙学常数影响下演化行为的研究结果，并将研究扩展至更一般的解空间，以获得对坍缩过程的更全面理解。

Method: 从一个初始静态构型出发，推导出控制系统时间演化的非线性二阶方程，并采用相平面分析（phase plane analysis）方法研究系统的时间行为，考察一般物理参数条件下系统的动力学特征。

Result: 在包含所有物理参数的一般情形下，系统会出现与非零电荷和宇宙学常数相关的折叠分岔（fold bifurcations）。研究澄清了此前的结果，并将分析扩展到完整的解空间。获得了对无剪切（shear-free）构型在坍缩过程中的时间演化的更全面理解。

Conclusion: 电磁场（非零电荷）和宇宙学常数的存在会引入折叠分岔，显著影响辐射相对论恒星在坍缩过程中的演化路径，扩展后的解空间分析提供了比此前研究更完整的动力学图景。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a radiating relativistic star in the presence of the electromagnetic field and the cosmological constant. The evolution of the model originates from an initially static configuration. The temporal behaviour of the system is governed by a nonlinear second order equation which is studied using a phase plane analysis. We show that in the general case, with all physical parameters present, fold bifurcations arise which are related to nonzero charge and cosmological constant. Our treatment clarifies earlier results and we extend our study to the general solution space. We obtain a more comprehensive understanding of the temporal evolution for a shear-free configuration during collapse.

</details>


### [164] [Thermal Quantum Fluctuations in Einstein-Nonlinear Maxwell-Yukawa Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02674)
*M. Mangut*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文研究了由Yukawa屏蔽电磁势生成的Einstein-非线性电动力学黑洞，并通过Bekenstein-Hawking熵上两种修正项（对数修正项和指数修正项）来分析热涨落对其热力学性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性电动力学黑洞的热力学性质，特别是通过不同修正项（对数项和指数项）来分析热涨落对标准热力学量的影响，以深入理解黑洞在不同尺度（大事件视界和小事件视界）下的热力学行为和稳定性。

Method: 该研究计算黑洞的基本热力学势和变量；在大事件视界情形下使用对数修正项（代表常数背景量子场的修正），在小事件视界情形下使用指数修正项（反映非微扰量子效应）添加到Bekenstein-Hawking熵中，然后通过图形分析所有情形下的热力学函数。

Result: 获得了所有修正情形下的热力学函数并通过图形详细讨论了黑洞的稳定性性质。

Conclusion: 通过对不同修正项的分析，系统揭示了Yukawa屏蔽电势影响下的Einstein-非线性电动力学黑洞在大、小事件视界两种情形下的热力学行为与稳定性特征。

Abstract: This study investigates an Einstein-nonlinear electrodynamic black hole generated by a Yukawa-screened electromagnetic potential and examines its thermal properties through the calculation of the fundamental thermodynamic potentials and variables. The effect of thermal fluctuations on the calculated standard thermodynamic quantities is analyzed through two different correction terms added to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. In this work, the thermodynamic behavior in cases where the black hole has a relatively large event horizon is investigated using a logarithmic correction term representing corrections arising from quantum fields in constant backgrounds. In addition, thermal fluctuations occurring in cases where the event horizon is small are analyzed through an exponential correction term added to the entropy, reflecting non-perturbative quantum effects. Finally, the thermodynamic functions for all obtained cases are examined graphically, and the stability properties of the black hole are discussed in detail.

</details>


### [165] [Ultralight Bosons Explain the Mass-Spin Correlations in the Merging Binary Black Hole Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02678)
*Xiao-Xiao Kou,Vuk Mandic,Ran Ding,Chi Tian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究超轻玻色子通过黑洞超辐射不稳定性形成的振荡云，提出一个包含环境诱导自旋变化的超辐射感知自旋分布模型，并将其与引力波瞬变目录（GWTCs）中合并黑洞双星种群观测进行比较。发现质量约mb~10^-12 eV的标量玻色子所预测的质量-自旋关系与GWTCs数据一致，贝叶斯因子从GWTC-3.0到5.0显著增长，在GWTC-5.0中达到ln B≈7.8，且该质量范围与此前对GW190728事件的波形分析研究高度重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻玻色子可能在快速旋转的黑洞周围触发超辐射不稳定性，从黑洞中提取转动能量并形成振荡云。作者希望利用引力波观测到的大量黑洞并合事件种群，检验这种超辐射机制在实际天体物理种群中的存在证据，从而间接探测超轻玻色子这一暗物质候选粒子。

Method: 作者构建了一个超辐射感知的自旋分布模型，该模型在标准黑洞自旋分布基础上额外考虑了超辐射不稳定性引起的环境诱导自旋变化（即当黑洞自旋落入超辐射敏感窗口时自旋被提取的效应）。将该模型预测的质量-自旋关系与GWTCs（GWTC-3.0至5.0）中观测到的合并黑洞双星种群进行贝叶斯模型比较。

Result: 标量玻色子质量约为mb~10^-12 eV时，其预测的黑洞质量-自旋分布与GWTCs观测数据吻合良好。统计学显著性随目录数据的增加而增强（从GWTC-3.0到GWTC-5.0），GWTC-5.0的贝叶斯因子达到ln B≈7.8（强有力证据）。该质量范围与此前基于GW190728引力波事件波形分析的研究结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 超辐射感知的自旋分布模型与不断扩充的并合黑洞双星种群数据高度兼容，为超轻玻色子（质量约10^-12 eV）在真实天体物理黑洞种群中触发超辐射不稳定性提供了有力的间接观测证据，也为探测轻暗物质候选粒子开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Ultralight bosons could trigger superradiant instabilities in rapidly spinning black holes, forming oscillating clouds while extracting rotational energy. We consider an extended, superradiance-informed spin distribution model that characterizes possible environment-induced spin variations and compare its predictions with the observed population of merging black hole binaries in the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalogs (GWTCs). We find that the mass-spin relation predicted by a scalar boson with mass $m_b\sim 10^{-12} \,\rm eV$ is consistent with the GWTCs, with increasing significance from GWTC-3.0 to 5.0. The Bayes factor reaches $\ln B \approx 7.8$ for GWTC-5.0. Intriguingly, this mass range largely coincides with a previous study based on a waveform analysis of the GW190728 gravitational wave event. Our findings provide compelling evidence that a superradiance-informed spin distribution model is highly compatible with the expanding binary black hole population dataset.

</details>


### [166] [Charged black-hole binary radiation at second post-Newtonian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02825)
*Andrea Placidi,Elisa Grilli,Matteo Pegorin,Marta Orselli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在2PN精度下研究了带电双星系统的电磁与引力辐射，推导了总能量通量、辐射多极矩及波形的电荷相关修正，并确定了新的电磁辐射记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已在2PN阶推导了带电双星系统的动力学，但尚未扩展到同一精度下的辐射领域。理解带电双星系统的辐射特征对于评估引力波信号中电荷的观测特征至关重要，因为带电双星会同时发射电磁和引力辐射。

Method: 扩展PN匹配的多极后闵可夫斯基（multipolar post-Minkowskian）方法和有效场论（EFT）方法到带电双星系统的辐射扇区，在2PN精度下独立计算辐射对称迹无关（STF）多极矩。

Result: 获得了2PN精度的带电双星总能量通量表达式，包括新的偶极主导电磁贡献及引力扇区中的电荷相关修正；两种独立形式主义（PN多极后闵可夫斯基与EFT）得到精确一致的结果；推导了2PN阶矢量场与引力场的辐射STF多极矩，并计算了波形球谐多极的电荷相关修正，包括由电磁辐射应力-能量张量触发的新记忆贡献。

Conclusion: 这些结果为构建精确的波形模型奠定了坚实基础，有助于评估引力波信号中电荷的观测特征。

Abstract: In this work, we build on Ref.~[Phys.~Rev.~D 112, 124060 (2025)], where we derived the dynamics of an electrically charged binary system at second post-Newtonian (2PN) order, by extending the analysis, at the same PN accuracy, to the associated emission of electromagnetic and gravitational radiation. We focus on non-spinning binaries that evolve quasi-adiabatically along a sequence of circular orbits, and compute the 2PN expression for the corresponding total energy flux at infinity. With respect to the well-known case of a neutral binary, the presence of charge induces both a new leading-order electromagnetic contribution of dipolar nature and additional charge-dependent corrections in the gravitational sector. To account for this richer phenomenology, we extend both the PN-matched multipolar post-Minkowskian and the effective field theory approaches to the radiation sector of charged binaries, with exact agreement for the contributions computed independently in both formalisms. We derive the radiative symmetric-trace-free multipole moments of the vector and gravitational fields at 2PN order. In doing so, we also compute 2PN-accurate charge-dependent corrections to the spherical multipoles of the waveform emitted by the binary, including a new memory contribution sourced by the stress-energy tensor of the electromagnetic radiation. These results lay the foundation for developing accurate waveform models capable of assessing the observational signatures of electric charge in gravitational-wave signals.

</details>
